KA RONG KA TAW KA SHAD 
KA KMEN U KHASI 


Ka Rong ka taw ne ka shad ka kmen ka long ka bynta 
kaba kongsan tam ha ka jingim u khun Khasi khara. U Kha^ 
u long uba dap da ka jingkmen bad sngewbha. U ieit ia ka 
lehkmen leh sngewbha, kum ka shad ka kmen, ka rwai ka 
siaw bad ka put ka tern. Haba ki briew ki leit sha lyngkha, 
ne haba Ieit tongum ne sait jain ne Ieit thohdieng phin iohsngew 
ba ki rwai ne kynud ne siaw da ki sur ba sngewtynnad. Wat 
ki khynnah ap masi sha lum ki ju rwai bad siaw sngewtynnad. 
Ki ju shna ki jingput da ki siej ne sla b2Kj pynmih ki sur kiba 
sngewtynnad. Ka jingitynnad ka mariang, ki pyntha bad ki 
them ki lum ryngkat bad ki dieng ki siej, ki syntiew ki skud ki 
nang pynitynnad shuh shuh ia kane ka Ri jong ngi, ka Ri ba 
shong ki Blei. 

Ki don ki jingiathuh khana puriskam ban pyni ha ngi 
ba ka Rong ka taw ka shad ka kmen ka la don naduh ki 
por barim bajah. Kawei na kita ki jingiathuh khana pateng 
ka iathuh ha ngi ba mynhyndai eh haba dang lung ka 
pyrthei, baroh ki mrad bad ki sim ki deuig nang kren. Te 
ha kawei ka sngi, haba katto katne ki mrad bad ki sim 
ki dang leit iaid kai, ki la lap ia u khun bynriew uba dang 
pynlong ka jingshad sngewbha ha kawei ka madan kaba 
heh, ryngkat bad ka put ka tern, ki bom ki ksing bzd ki 
put tangmuri. Ki mrad bad ki sim ki la itynnad palat ia

kane ka jingshad u khun bynriew, bad ki ruh ki la ia kut 
jingmut ban pynlong ka jingshad kaba kum kane. Kumta, 
ki la pynbeit ia ka sngi ba kin pynlong ka shad ka kmen 
kum ka jong u khun bynriew. Haba la poi kata ka sngi 
baroh ki jaid sim jaid mrad ha la ka riam ka belt ki la ia 
poi sha ka madan shad. Ki la ia sdang ka put ka tern, 
ka shad ka kmen. Ha kata ka por, la wan poi u kui bad 


la ka waitlam kaba itynnad bad kaba phymai haduh katta 
katta. U la sdang ban shad baroh ki la iapbicit ia ka 
jingshad jong u, bad u la sngew sarong shibun eh. Ha kata 
ka por, la wan poi u pyrthat bad u la pan kylliang ia ka 
waitlam na u kui. U pyrthat u la sdang shad bad ka talain 
artad ia ka waitlam, bad u la kiew soit sha sahit bneng 
da kaba rah iang ia ka waitlam jong u kui. U Kui u la 
sngewsih palat, u pyrshemg ban ioh biang ia ka waitlam, 
hynrei u l^lem ioh shuh, naduh kata ka por sa sdang ka 
jingiashun hapdeng ki mrad baphrr baphcr. 

Sa kawei pat ka jingiathuh khana ka ong ba mynhyndai 
eh, baroh ki jaid sim, bad laiphew mrad ki la iapynbeit ban 
long ka jingshad. Haba la poi kata ka sngi baroh ki la ia 
mih ha la ki riam ki beit kiba itynnad. U Shakyrlia u put 
tangmuri. U Diengkhied u tern ki kynshaw, bad u risang 
u tern bom. Ka jingpul jingtem jong ki ka la sawa haduh 
katta katta, bad ki sim ki mrad ki nang ia bun ia bun. 
Ki mrad bad ki sim ki la ia shad baroh shisngi haduh ba 
ki la thait. Hamar ba ki mut ban ia leit phai noh wan mih 
ka Sngi bad u Bnai kiba ia long shipara, bad ki la iasdang 
ban shad. Baroh kiba peit kai, ki ong ba ka long khlem 
akor ba kin iashad shipara. Ka Sngi ba la sngew lehrain, 
bad ka la phet da kaba sngewrem bad leit rieh ialade ha 
ka krem Lamet Ka Krem Latang. Kumta la tap ia ka 
pyrthei da ka jingdum. Bun napdeng ki mrad bad ki sim

kiba la ia leit ban khroh ia ka ban wan phai biang, hynrei 
ka sngi kam treh. Khatduh eh la mih u iar Ryngkuh, bad 
u la ong ba un leit shaw hi ma u ia ka sngi. Ka sngi ka 
la iathuh lut ia kiei kiei kiba la jia baroh . kumno ba ki 
la ia rkhie bein ia ka. U syiar u la ong ia ka ba nadien 
nangne kin nym rkhie bein shuh ia ka. Ka sngi ka la 
kohnguh ban wan biang tad ynda ka iohsngcw ba u syiar 
un kynih laisien. Kumta sa mih biang ka sngi bad ka 
jingshai ha ka pyrthei. 

Kine ki long ki khanatang jong ka Ri jong nqi kiba 
la iathuh pateng la pateng, hynrei ki don Iang bad ki 


jingmut bad symboh pyrkhat ban pyni ba ka shad ka kmcn 
ka put ka tern ka la don naduh mynhyndai ch ha u Khasi. 

Don shibun ki rong ki taw ne ka shad ka kmen kiba 
u khun Khasi u ju pynlong man la u snem. Don kiba iasnoh 
bad ka lehniam leh nikom, don pat kiba shu shad sngewbha. 
La ju pynlong ia ki ha shuwa ban bet ia u symbai ha ki 
lyngkha ne ynda la dep ot. Ka shad suk Mynsiem ne shad 
Weiking kam don jinglehniam ei ei, ki shu dwai ha u Blei 
Trai kynrad ban bha ban miat ban koit ban khiah, ban bha 
ka rep ka riang, bad pan na u Blei ban ioh ia ka jingim 
suk im sain baroh shisnem. Hynrei ka shad Nongkrem ka 
don bad ka lehniam. Don ki Rong ki Taw ba la pynbeit 
da ki shnong, don pat kiba ia pynbeit da ki Raid, bad ki 
Hima. U Khasi u don ka jingiajan bha bad ka mariang bad 
kiei kiei kiba don sawdong jong ka. Ka rep ka riang ka 
long ka kam ba kongsan tarn jong u briew, kumta u ieit 
bad burom ia ka khyndew kaba pynmih ia ki jingthung 
jingtep bad pynkdctng ia ki briew. Kumta ha kino kino ki 
rong ki taw, kim ju klet ban duwai ha U Blei Trai Kynrad 
Nongbuh Nongthaw ban kyrkhu kyrdoh, ban bha ka rep ka

riang, ka Ieit ka wan, ka synshar ka khadar, ban ioh ka 
jingkoit jingkhiah, ban lait na ka khlam ka ngoh, na ka 
thma bad ka nem sniew, ban bha ki nongsynshar, u syiem 
u kmie, kiba khraw bad ki briew baroh. Kim ju klet ruh 
ban ainguh ia u Blei Trai kynrad na ka bynta ki jingkyrkhu 
bad jingbha kiba u la theh halor jong ki. 

Ngin shu shim khyndiat tylli ki rong ki taw ba kongsan 
jong u khun khasi khara. 

(1) Ka shad shiphiah ne Shad long Nikhla ne shad 
Laho : 

Ka jingshad Laho ka long ka jingshad kaba la rim bha 
jong ki Pnar ha Ri lum Jaintia. Ka kyrteng tynrai jong ka 
ka dei ka shad shiphiah, ban phiah ia ka jingshad hapdeng 
arngut ki shynrang, u wei sha kadiang bad u wei sha 
kamon bad ka kynthei ka shad hapdeng. Ka shad shiphiah 
ka iasoh bad ka jingsdang jong ka jaidbynriew. Hadien ba 


u Hynniew-wasa ne Hynniew Trep u la sah noh ha kane 
ka F>yrthei, u la kwah ban pynlong ka jingshad ainguh ia 
u Biel Trai Kynrad Nongbuh Nongthaw. La ia pan da kaba 
suit kaba shor ia ka shad shi jur, hynrei ka dak ka shin 
kam paw ba U Blei u mon ia ka shad shijur, u wei u 
shynrang bad kawei ka kynthei. Ki la ia pan da kaba shad 
shiphiah kumta ka dak ka shin ka pynpaw ba U Blei u mon 
ia kane ka jingshad. La iathuh ba ka shad shiphiah ka la 
jan duh noh, bad ym shym lah ban pynlong la slem bah. 
Kumta ka kur Nikhla ka la pynkhie im biang ia kane ka 
jingshad, bad la khot ia ka shad shiphiah da ka shad iong 
Nikhla. Ka kyrteng Laho ka mih hadien na ki nongrwai 
kiba ju rwai. "Hooh ka la Hooh". Ka shad shiphiah ka long 
ka jingshad ainguh ia U Blei Trai kynrad na ka bynta ki

jingbha bad jingkyrkhu kiba u la theh sei ha ki briew. 

2. Ka Rongkhli : 

Ka Rongkhli ne Rongkhla ka long ka jingleh niam 
ryngkat bad ka shad ka kmen jong ki war Jaintia ha 
Nongtalang, kaba don ha khap Bangladesh. Ki ju pynlong 
ia ka Rongkhli ha kano kano ka por ba ki ioh pyniap ia 
u khla. Lada ioh pyniap ia u khla ha kano kano ka rukom, 
da kaba siat, ne ha kaba leit beh mrad ne ha kano kano 
ka rukom jingpyniap, ki dei ban pynlong ia la ka lehniam 
leh rukom ban ainguh ia U Blei. Lada kim leh ia ka niam 
ka rukom, ki ngeit ba kin wan hap ki jingjia kiba sniew, 
kum ka pang ka jhia, ka jingmatlah, ka jingiap thngan bad 
kiwei kiwei de. Ka jingpynlong ia ka lehniam Rongkhli ka 
iong kat kum ba ioh pyniap ia u khla, ki ju leh ha ki bnai 
Kyilalyngkot, Rymphang ne Lber. 

3. Ka Behdeinkhlam : 

Ka Behdeinkhlam ka long kawei pat ka jingleh niam 
jong ki Pnar. Ka Behdeinkhlam ha Jowai ka dei ka 
jinglehniam jong ka Raid Jowai. Ki ju pynlong ruh ia ka 
Behdeinkhlam ha Id Raid Tuber, Shyrmang, lalong, Mukhla 
bad Muthlong. la kane ka lehniam la ju pynlong ha ki bnai 
lyiur kum u Jylliew bad Naitung, ha ka |x>r ba la dep bet 


lut ia u symbai. Ka jingthmu ba kongsan jong ka 
Behdeinkhlam ka long ban ainguh ia U Blei Trai Kynra ba 
la theh kyrhai ki jingkyrkhu. Ha ka juh ka por ban kyipad 
ia u ban lait na ka khlam ka ngoh, ka pang ka jhia, bad 
kiwei kiwei ki jingsniew kiba wan ha ka jingim jong ki 
briew. Ki duwai nih ban bha ka .rep ka riang bad ba ki 
briew kin im suk im sain baroh shisnem.

4. Ka Lukhmi : 

Ka Lukhmi ka long ka lehniam ha raid lapngar ha 
thain Bhoi. Ki ju pynlong ia kane ka lehniam ha u bnai 
Lber, shuwa ban bet ia u ^nmbai bad haba la dep ot ia 
u kba. Kane ka jinglehniam la ju leh na ka bynta ka 
Lukhmi ne ka ‘Leikhaspah kaba pynbha ia ka rep ka riang 
kaba long ka spah bah ia ki briew ha lone ki thain, namar 
bun na ki, ki im da ka rep ka riang. Ki ju lehniam ban 
bha ka rep ka riang khamtam u kba, bad na ka bynta ka 
jingbha jingmiat jong ki briew baroh, khnang ba kin lah ban 
im bha im miat baroh shisnem. 

5. Ka Sajer ka Raid Nonglyngdoh : 

Ka Sajer ruh ka long kawei pat ka jinglehniam ha ka 
Raid Nonglyngdoh ha thain Bhoi. La ju pynlong ia ka ha 
u bnai Nohpiah, Kyllalyngkot ne Rymphang haba la dep 
ot kba. Ki pynlong ia kane ka jinglehniam ban bha ka rep 
ka riang, khamtam u kba, uba long ka jingbam ba kongsan 
jong ki Khasi. W duwai ainguh ruh ia U Blei u Trai Kynrad 
Nongbuh Nongthaw na ka bynta baroh ki jingkyrkhu kiba 
u la theh sei halor jong ki. 

6. Ka Lakympong : 

Ha katto katne ki Raid ha thain Bhoi kum ka Raid 
Tyrso bad Flaid Nongtung ki ju pynlong ia ka Lakympong. 
Don ar jaid ki Lakympong, kawei ka dei ka Lakympong 
khynraw bad kawei pat ka Lakympong Lukhmi. Kaba 
nyngkong kam donniam, ka dei ka jingshad sngewbha jong 
ki samla, don kiba ong ba ka Lalqnnpong ka dei ka Lei 
jingieit. Ki ju pynlong ia kane ka Lakympong ha u bnai

Rymphang ne Lber, haba la dep ka aiom ot, bad shuwa 
ban bet ia u symbai ba thymmai. Ka Lakympong Lukhmi 
ka don lang bad ka lehniam, ia kane la ju pynlong shisien 
san snem. Ki lehniam Lakympong Lukhmi na ka bynta ban 
bha ka rep ka riang khamtam u kba u khaw, ban bha ka 
leit ka wan ban ioh ka koit ka khiah. 

7. Ka Pomblang Syiem ne Pomblang Nongkrem : 

Ka Pomblang Syiem ne Pomblang Nongkrem ne 
Shad Nongkrem ka long ka jinglehniam ba shisien shisnem 
jong ka Hima Khyrim. Ki ju pynlong ha Ingsad ha Smit, 
kaba long ka Ingniam bad ka jaka shong ka Syiem Sad bad 
ki Syiem jong ka Hima Khyrim. La ju pynlong ia kane ka 
lehniam ban kynmaw bad burom ia ki longshuwa, ki 
nongseng ia ka niam bad ka hima. Ki ju duwai ha u Blei 
Trai kynrad ban theh shibun ki jingkyrkhu na ka bynta ban 
bha ka rep ka riang, ban bha ka leit ka wan, ban bha ban 
miat ki s^nem, kiba khraw bad ki khun ki hajar, ban ioh 
ia ka shongsuk shongsain baroh shisnem. 

8. Ka Shad Suk Mynsiem ne Shad Weiking : 

Ka Shad Suk Mynsiem ne Shad Weiking ka long ka 
jingshad ba shisien shisnem ba la pyniaid da ka seng Khetsi. 
Ka long ka jingshad ha kaba ki briew ki ainguh ia u Blei 
Trai kynrad Nongbuh Nongthaw na ka bynta ki jingkyrkhu 
kiba la ai ha ki na ka bynta baroh shisnem, bad ban duwai 
ia u Blei Trai Kynrad ban theh kyrhai ki jing kyrkhu ban 
bha ka rep ka riang, ban im suk imsain baroh shisnem. 
Ka shad Suk Mynsiem ka ju long man u snem ha u bnai 
laiong. 

Dang don bun shuh shuh ki rong ki taw, ka shad ka

kmen ha kiwei pat ki bynta jong ka Ri. Ka shad phur 
Nongjri, Sohbar kaba ki ju lehniam ia ki nongiap. Ka shad 
Lyngdoh Wahkhen, ka phur ki khatar shnong, ka shad 
Thung Syiem Thang Syiem ha Sohra. Don ruh sa ka shad 
Raid Mamgar, ka shad krud kba bad shad Longhai, ka 
shad Kynthei ne Nongkharai, ka on pepbah ne ka shad 


phur shad siang ka Hima Maharam ka shad krud ld>a bad 
shad kylla Mohkhiew ha Warmawsaw bad Patharkhmah, ka 
shad Raid Shabong ha Pynursla, ka shad Nguh Meikha ha 
Raid lapngar. Nangta, don nih sa ka shad Mastieh Behkhla 
ki Mynso, ka shad phur ki Lamin, ka shad Sukra ha 
Nongbah, ka shad kynthei ki Nongwataw ka shad Behmiad 
ka thain lum Diengiei bad sa kiwei kiwei de. 

Naduh ki por barim bajah, u khun bynriew u ju ku/ah 
ban pynpaw shabar ia ki jingsngewbha bad jingsngewsih, 
ki jingmut ki jingpyrkhat, ki jingsngew ba bunjait iyngba 
kino Idno ki iad, kum ka jingoh dur, kaba dro, ka rvuai, bad 
ka put ka tern. Kumta ka rong ka taw, ka shad ka kmen 
ka dei kawei na ki iad kiba kongsan, ha kaba u briew u 
pynpaw ia ki jingsngew, ka jinglong tynrai, kaei kaei kaba 
u khein kordor bad kaba u kwah bad sliang ban pynneh 
pynsah bad pynim. Kumta, wat la u Khasi um don ka thoh 
ka tar naduh mynbafim, na ka daw ba u la nguid noh ia 
ka kot kat kum ka jingiathuh khana pateng, hyndai u don 
ka jingstad bad jingshemphang. Ka History jong u Kheisi ka 
don bad paw shynna, ha ki Khanatang ki puriskam bad 
purinam. Id jingduwai, ki phawar. Id jingrwai, ha ka lehniam 
leh rukom, ha ka shad ka kmen. la kine la pynneh pynszJi 
pateng la pateng. 

Ka rong ka taw ka shad ka kmen ka long ka bynta 
kaba khraw bad kaba kyntcing ha ka jingim u Khasi. Ki

pynphalang ia ka imlang sahiang, ka niam ka mkom, ka 
synshar ka khadar bad ka>,ioh ka kot. Ki pyni ruh kumno 
u Khasi u long u ba tip briew tip blei, u burom ia ki baurim 
bad baiap. Ki pyni ruh ba kumno ki Syiem, ki Lyngdoh, 
Doloi, ki Myntri, ki Bakhraw ki don ki bynta bapher bapher 
ha kaba pynlong ia kine ki rong ki taw ki shad ki lonen 
ba bunjaid. Ka bynta jong ka syiem Sad kum ka nongbat 
ia ka niam ka rukom ka pynphalang ia ka jinglong ka 
jaidbynriew ba ka burom ia ka kynthei kum ka nongri ka 
nongkdup bad nongsumar ia ka niam ka rukom, ka ing ka 
sem bad ka kur ka jaid. 


Dei ha ka por ba pynlong ia ki rong ki taw ka shad 
ka kmen ba ki briew ki iohlad ban ia kynduh ia kiba haing 
hasem, ki kur ki jaid ki kha ki man. Kito kiba don khaum 
sha jngai mh ki iohlad ban weui ia kynduh ia la kiba haing 
ha sem. Ki rong ki taw kim dei tang ka rukom lehniam, 
hsmrei ki dei ki bynta ba kordor ha ka imbng sahlang. Ka 
dei ka por ban sei ki khor ki khriam, ki dhara, ki p)aila, 
ki ^gdeng ksiar, jingdeng rupa. Ka dei ka por ban pyni 
ia la ki khun kynthei samla, namar ki kynthei kiba shad 
Id dei ban long sotti. Lada don uno uno u rang uba iapmat 
iano ieuio u leth ban phah kylli bad betn ia poikha poiman. 

Ka jingshad kynthei bad shynrang ka pyni ia ka bynta 
jong ki ha ka imlang sahlang. Ki shynrang ki ju shad 
barabor sharud bad ki kynthei ki ju shad hapdeng. Kane 
ka pyni ba u shynrang u dei u nongker u nongda ia ka 
kynthei ka khynnah, ka ing ka sem, ka kur ka jaid, ka Ri 
bad ka jaidbynriew. Ka jingshad jong ki kynthei hapdeng 
bad pyndem ia ki khmat shapoh, ka pyni ia ka jinglui lui, 
ka jinglong sotti bad jingsngewrit, ki jinglong ba ka kynthei 
ka dei ban don. Ka jingker ia ki kynthei hapdeng ka 
isynpaw ruh ia ka kyrdan bad burom kaba ka jaidbynriew

ka ai ia ki kynthei. La niew burom ia ka kynthei kum ka 
nongri nongsumar, ka nonglum ka nongiang, ka nongpynroi 
ia u tnum u tyndcii, ka kur ka jaid, ka Ri bad ka jaidbynriew. 

Ha ka jingshad Laho, ju don laingut ki nongshad, 
amgut ki shsmrang, uwei sha ka diang bad uwei p)at sha 
ka mon, ka kynthei ka shad hapdeng. Ki amgut ki shynrang 
ka thew ia u Kni u ba long u nongker nongda ia ka ing 
ka sem bad ka kur ka jaid, uwei pat ka mut ia u lok uba 
long u kpa ki khun bad ka ing. Ka jingker ia ka kynthei 
hapdeng ka pynpaw ia ka bad ka ing. Ka jingker ia ka 
kynthei hapdeng ka pynpaw ia ka burom bad kyrdan ba 
la ai ha ka. Ka Nongshad kynthei ka shad marwei, ka thew 
ia ka 'Langdoh' kaba long ka nongbat ia ka niam ka rukom. 

Ka Shadwait bad shadmastieh ki long ki- rukom 
shad thma ban pynpaw ia ka jingkmen hadien ba la jop 


thma. Ha ka riam shad shynrang, ki ju don ka Ryngkap 
bad ki khnam ban pyni ia ka kamram jong ki shynrang 
kum u nongda u nongker. 

Haba don ka shad ka kmen ka iarap ruh ia ka ioh 
ka kot, ki briew ki thied ki jain thymmai bad kiwei tdwei 
de ki tiar ki tar ban pynphuh pynphieng ia ka ing ka sem. 
Ki ju pynkhuid nih ia ki ing ki sem, ki lynti ki syngkieng, 
ban itynnad, kumta don ki jingkhuid ha ki shnong ki thaw. 
Ha ka por shad Nongkrem haba ngi leit sha Smit, ngi ju 
iohi baheh bha ka die ka thied, mih bun ki dukan ja dukan 
sha, ki soh ki pai, bad kaba sngew tynnad eh long ka ban 
leit bam sohphlang sha Smit. 

U Khasi u long uba tipbriew tipblei, tip kur tip kha, 
u long ruh uba ieit ia ka jinglaitluid. Ha Ri Pnar ki iaieh

pyrshah ia ki Phareng namar ba ki pyrshang ban pynwit 
ia ka rukom thangbriew ha Jowai, bad mushlia ia ka 
jingshad kaba la pynlong ha Jalong, kumta la khie ka thma 
hapoh ka jingialam u Kiang Nangbah, bad u la sheJ) sdien 
phasi ha ka 30th u Nohprah, 1862. 

Ka rong ka taw ka shad ka kmen ka pyniajatn bad 
tehsong ia ka jaidbynriew. Ka wanrah iaka jingkmen 
jingsngewbha, ka pynphuh pynphieng ia ka jaidbynriew. 
Haba la poi ki sngi jong ka Pomblang Nongkrem, ka 
Behdienkhlam, ka Shad Weiking bad kiwei kiwei de, baroh 
ki ia kmen bad ki ia leit ban peit ia kine ki jinglehniam 
lehrukom bad ka shad ka kmen. Kumta katba dang neh 
ki rong ki taw, ka shad ka kmen dang phuhmut phuhmat 
ka jaidbynriew neimar ki pynphalang ia ka jingshad bad png 
shemphang jong ka jaidbyniew Khasi. 


U KHASl BAD KA PUT KA TEM 
KA RWAI KA SlAW 


Kham niar eh ban don ki briew ki bym sngewtynnad 
ia ka put ka tern, ka rwai ka siaw. Wat lada u briew um 
nang satia ban rwai ne ban put ban tern, hynrei u 
sngewtynnad pat ban iohsngew ia ki sur jingrwai bad 
jingput jingtem. Haba don ka put ka tern ka nvai ka siaw, 
ryngkat bad ki sur ba sngewtynnad hangta don ka jingieit 
ka jingsuk, ka jingkmen, ka jingsngewbha bad jingsngew 
sih. Ki sur jingrwai ki pynkmen bad pynkmen ia ka dohnud 
briew. 1 Bah LG.Shullai, ha ka kot kaba I la pynmih ia 
ki jingrwai jong I Bah Teslet Pariat (Bam Kwai ha ling U 
Biei) kaba I la ai kyrteng, "Ka Piiang Rupa" I la buh ia kine 
ki kyntien :

"Don ki sur kiba thiang, ha ki lum bad ki wah, 
Ha jingkhih jong ki sder, ki sla dieng bad ki khah, 
Ha jingpah jong ki sim, bad jingkynud ki ngap, 
Ha jingnoh jong ki kshaid bad jingriew ki umslap, 
Ha mgi ne dymmiew, ha ki thwei ne ki khriang, 
Ngi lah ban iohsngew ia ki sur kiba thiang. 

Ki jingrwai kiba la thaw bha, kiba biang ka dur bad 
ka sur, ki ngam sha ka dohnud briew bad ktah ia ka 
jingsngew jong u. Haba ngi don hapdeng ka jingrwai ba 
sngewsih, ngi sngew kum ban jaw ummat, namar kita ki 
kyntien bad sur ki ktah ia ka dohnud ba sngewsih, teng 
teng ki ummat ruh ki tuid la ka tuid hi. Ki jingrwai ki don 
bun jaid, don ki jingrwai bam ainguh ia u Trai Nongthaw, 
ki jingrwai ieit Ri, ki jingrwai kmen ne sngewsih. jingrwai 
ieit ia la ki kmie ki kpa ne jingieit samla, jingrwai kynoi 
thiah, jingrwai thamula ne danda, jingrwai pharshi, ki jingrwai 


ban laihuh khana shaphang ki puriskam bad ki purinatn, 
bad ki phawar ba bun jaid, kum ki phawar iasiat, phawar 
behmrad, phawar sngewbha, phawar iambriew bad kiwei 
kiwei de. 

Don ka jingong ha ka ktien Phareng "Music ia an 
international language, it has no barrier and no frontier", 
Ki jingrwai bad ka put ka tern, ki dei ki ktien jong ka 
pyrthei, ym don jingker, ym don u pud. Lyngba jong ki, 
ki briew ha kano kano ka bynta jong ka pyrthei ki lah ban 
sngewthuh. Haba rwai da kano kano ka jaid ktien, ngi ia 
pynkhih kjat, pynkhih khlieh. Naduh ki khyllung ki khynnah, 
bad ki samla, haduh kiba la san la rangbah, naduh kiba 
nang kiba stad, haduh kiba bieit kiba them, ki sngewtynnad 
ia ka put ka tern, ka rwai ka siaw.

Ki Khasi ki don ka sap rwai, bad ki nang bha ban 
rwai, ryngkat bad ka sur kaba sngewtynnad. Ki nang ruh 
ban put bad tern shibun ki jaid jingput jingtcm. Wat ki 
khynnah ap masi sha lum, ki rwai ki siaw da ki sur kiba 
sngewthiang bad sngewtynnad ki ju put da ki sla, ki siej, 
ki japung bad ki pynmih ki sur bathiang. Ki briew ki ju 
kynud da ki sur ba sngewtynnad, kiba ia hap lang bad ka 
jingitynnad jong ki lum ki wah ki kshaid ba noh rymphum, 
ki wah bad ki wahduid, ki khlaw ki btap, ki syntiew ki 
skud, ki sim ki doh bad ki mrad. Dei kane ka jingitynnad 
jong ka mariang lehse, kaba la pynlong ia u sardar V. Patel 
haba u wan sha Shillong ha ka snem 1948, ban ong ba 
kane ka dei ka Ri ba shong ki Blei. 

Ha ki por mynshuwa, bad mynta ruh lehse, ha ki 
nongkyndong jong ngi ki briew ynda ki la dep trei dcp 
ktah, dep bam dep dih, ki ju ia shong sawdong ka lyngwiar 
dpei. Ki ju ia thuh ki jingiathuh khana kiba sngewtynnad 
shaphang ki puriskam bad ki purinam. Don kiba ju rwai 
sngeaiynnad da kaba tern iaki duitara bad marynthing. 


Kum ka jaidbynriew, ngi don ki jingrwai bad tiar put tiar 
tern tynrai na iajong. Ki tiar put tiar tern ki kynthup ia ki 
Bisli, ki Tangmuri, ki sharati, ki Marynthing bad ki Duitara, 
ki Bom ki Nakra bad ki ksing. Ki jingrwai ruh ki don 
bunjaid kumba la kdew haneng. Ka jingwan jong ki Phareng 
ryngkat bad ka jingnang ka jingstad, ki la pynkylla ruh ia 
ka rwai ka siaw ka put ka tern Khasi, ki jingrwai ki jingput 
jingtem ki la kylla long kum ka jong ki nongsepngi 
(Westernised). Hynrei ki briew ha ki nongkyndong ki dang 
sngewtynnad hi ia ka put ka tern ia jong. 

Katno ka long kaba sngewtynnad bad sngew sarong, 
ba ki briew jong ngi kin nang bad bud ia ka jong kiwei pat.

Hynrei ka dei ruh ia ki nongrwai bad nongput nongtem jong 
ngi, ba kin nang bad tip ia la ki jong ki jingrwai bad jingput 
jingtem la jong ha ki sur ba nylla. Kum kiwei pat, ki jaid 
bynriew ha ka Ri India jong ngi, katno ki khein kor ia ki 
tabla, ki sitar ki harmonium bad kiwei kiwei pat. Ki da buh 
bha ia ki bad ki ju phah ruh ia ki khun ki kti ban leit hikai 
ban nang ia ki. I bah E.B.R.Wanswet, ha ka kot kaba I ai 
kyrteng "Ki ktien Thieing Snah", I ong ba "donkam ban lum 
thiar kloi kloi ia ki thymmei sur ia ka tern ka put, U khynriam, 
U pnar, U Bhoi U War. Kum u nongrwai, nongthaw jingrwai 
bad nongput nongtem u la tbit bha, U bah Break U buh ruh 
sa kinc ki kyntien ha ka kot jong u, 

Ka jaidbynriew kan ieng Rasong 
Haba ka tip tynrai la jong" 

Ngi sngewkmen ba ka All India Radio, ki seng Bhalang 
kum ka Hynniew Trep Cultural and Welfare Organisation 
(HTCWO) etc. ki ju pynlong ka jingiakop jingrwai tynrai, bad 
kumjuh ruh ka Seng Khasi, Sein Raj, Rympei Theatrical Centre 
kiba pynshlur ia ka put ka tern iajong. 

Ka long kaei kaba sngewtynnad lada lah ban pynlong 


ki jingiakop jingrwai, ka put ka tem. Kum kine ki jingiakop 
ki ai lad ia ki samla, ban ia shim bynta, ban pyni ia la 
ki sap ki phong kaba ki don, bad pynshlur mh ia ki samla 
ban lait na ki jinglehrain biej. Lada ki samla, ki sngewtynnad 
ia ka rwai bad ka put ka tern, ym da kaba pynlap miet 
palat, kin lait mh na ki jingsniew kiba bun. Ngi kyrmen ba 
ka Ri jong ngi kan nang pynmih shibun ki nongrwai bad 
nongput nongtem, ki ban pyndonburom ia ka*Ri bad ka 
jaid bynriew. Ngim dei ban long ka jaidbynriew kumba la 
rwai Pharshi da I Beh T.T.Mukhim "Pyrthci ka juk mynta

don pisa te I jongnga", ngin kynmaw ia ki kyntien ksiar 
kyntien mpa jong I nongrwai bad nongput nongtem ba 
pawnam "I Bah C.D.Lyngdoh (bam kwai.ha iing U- Blei) 
kiba ong "Katba dang tip i 'u Trai Nongthaw, ko Ri jong’ 
nga phcun nym long mraw".

Ka jaidbynriew jongngi kalong ka jaidbynriew ba 
kyrpang kaba tipbriew tipblei, tip kur tip kha, bad kaba 
la {}ynshong nongrim ha ka ban kamai ia ka hok. Ka don 
la ki jong ki riti‘ ki dustur, ka ktien ka thylliej, ka niam ka 
mkom, ka riam ka beit bad ka mkom synshar paidbah. Ka 
jinglong ba kyrpang jong ka jaidbynriew jongngi ka long ka 
jingshimjaid bad hiatpateng na ka kmie, kaba ngi khot ha 
ka ktien phareng ka "matrilineal society". Ngim dei ka 
'matriarchal society' kaba mut mh ka jingsynshar ne longfchlieh 
da ka kmie. Ka jaidbynriew jongngi ka ai burom bad 
pynshongdor ia baroh bad kynthei bad shynrang. Ki 
longshuwa manshuwa jongngi da ka jingstad bad 
jingshemphang jong ki, ki la buh lypa ia ka kyrdan jong ki 
kynthei bad shynretng bad baroh ar ki don la ka jong- ka 
bynta ha ka ing ka sem bad ka imlang sahlang. U shynrang 
u long u lok bad u kpa ki khun ha ing khun, uba bsa uba 
btiah bad pyndap pynbiang ia ka ing ka sem, bad uba ai 
ia ka long rynieng ia ki khun. Ha ing kur pjat u long u kni 
uba p>eit ia .ka kam iap kam im, u long ruh u nongbtin 
lynti, ia ka kur ka jetid, bad u sneng u kraw ia ki para ki 
pyrsa. U pynbeit ia ki jingiakajia bad sa kiwei kiwei pat

ki kam. U rangbah u dei ruh u nongker u nongda ia ka 
ing ka sem, ka kur ka jaid, ka Ri bad ka jaidbynriew. Ka 
kynthei ka long ka nongri nongsumar ia ka ing ka sem, ki 
khun ki kti ka nongseng ka nongpun, ka nongpynroi ia u 
tnum u tyndai bad ka nongbat ia ka jaid ka khong pateng 
la pateng. Ka khatduh ka don ka bynta kaba khraw shibun 




ch. Ka dei ban peit ia ki kmie ki kpa bad ia kivuei pat 
ki hynmen ki para kiba shem ha ka jynjar ka lanot.- bad 
kumjuh ruh ban pyndep ia ka niam ka rukom. Kumta ia 
ka iing khatduh la khot ruh ka ingseng, ka iingniam nc ka 
ing kur. Ka jingioh pateng jong ka khatduh kam tnut satia 
ba ka lah ban leh katba ka mon khlem ka jingiatip 1cm 
ki kni, ki hynmen para bad kiba ha ing ha sem, namar 
ka khatduh ka long tang ka nongri nongsumar bad ka 
• nongpeit ka nongkhmih (custodian). 

Ngim lah ban len ba shibun ki jingkylla ki la wan ha ka 
Ri bad ka jaidbynriew jong ngi. Mynta ngi dang iaid lyngba ia 
ka por jong ki jingkylla kaba ki ong ha ka ktien phareng ka 
"Transitory period". Ngi hap ban ia kynduh bad tyngkhuh 
bad shibun ki jingkylla ha ka imlang sahlang. Kine ki long, ka 
jingwan Id jaidbynriew bad kolshor bapher bapher, ka jingroi 
ka jingpar, ka ioh ka kot, ka ^shar ka khadar, ka jingkiew 
ka jingnang ka jingstad, ka jingiaid stet jong ka Science bad . 
Technology. Tcng teng ngim sngewlah ban shemphang bad 
sngewthuh shuh. Ki jingsniew kiba bun ki la nang kynrei ha 
ka imlang sahlang, ka kiad bad jingdih buaid, ka khalai ka 
jingpathar ka shongkha ka shongman kiba la pynjot pynpra 
ia bun ki briew bad ki ing ki sem. Nangta sa ki drugs, & 
kynhun leh noh bad ka jingkwah sp>ah bad ia kiei kiei ki jong 
ka pyrthei (materialism), ka jingkwah spcth kyrkieh bad da

kaba suk khlem trei shitom (easy money), ka bamsap (cor- 
ruption) ki la wan bsuh ha ka jaidbynriew jongngi. Ka jingwan 
jong ki television, video, cable network, sa ki kot bad 
jingpynphriang khubor ba bunjaid (literature & media) bad sa 
ki kot bunjaid kiba jakhlia (pornographic literature) ki la

Man la ka sngi, haba ngi pule kot khubor, peit ia ki 


Television, sngap iaki radio, ngi iohi tang ka thma, ka 
^giap, ka jingban bein, ka jingiap thngan, ka jingpynjot 
ka mariang. Ngi iohi ruh tang ka jingbishni jingpihuin, 
jingbamsap, jingbym sngewthuh jingmut, ka jingsngew tang 
iaiade, ka jingleh bor bad thombor, ka jingsngewpher ka 
niam, ka jeudbynriew bad ka ktien, ka jingbuaid bor bad 
kumta ter ter. Kine ki ktah bha ia ki khynnah bad samla 
kiba dang khie dang san kiba iohi ia kine man ka sngi. 
Dei ha kine ki por inynta, ba mangi kum ki nongshong 
shnong, ngi don ka bynta kaba khia shibun eh ha ka ban 
tei ia ka longbriew manbriew, ka ing ka sem, ka kur ka 
jaid, ka shnong ka thaw, ka Ri bad ka jaidbynriew. 

U John Milton, u nongthoh bapawnam u ong "good 
and evil, we know in the field of this world grow up 
together almost inseparably", kaba mut, ba ka jingbha bad 
ka jingsniew ha ka lyngkha jong kane ka pyrthei ki ia san 
ryngkat lang bad ym lah ban pyniakhlad ia ki. Kumta haba 
don ka jingbha don ruh ka jingsniew, hynrei ka dei pat ka 
kamram jongngl ban nym ai lad ba kaba sniew kan thombor 
ia kaba bha, bad ban peit barabor ia kaba bha bad leh ia 
kaba bha.

Ngin ia phai khyndiat halor katto katne ki mat kum 
ki symboh pyrkhat : 

(1) Ka thyinniei jong kiei kiei baroh ka dei ka iing: 

Ka ing ka dei ka thymmei jong kiei kiei baroh. 
Kumta, ngi dei ban tei ia ka ing ha ka nongrim k<tba 
skhem jong ka jingieit, ka jingshaniah bad ka jingsngew 
thuh jingmut, namar dei Idne kiban pynkhlain bad pyilskhem 
ia ki ing ki sem johgngi. 

Haba ngi ia nujor ia ki kynthei bad longkmie jongngi, 
ngi shem ba ki don ka bynta kaba khraw shibun eh. Katba ki 
kynthei ha kylleng ka pyrthei bad ha Ri India jong ngi ruh, ki 
ia leh ban ioh ia ka jinglaithiid, ki kynthei jong ngi ki la ioh 


lypa. Kat shaba ki iaid ki ieng, ki trei ki ktah, la ha ing ne 
shabar, ki leh da ka jingheh mynsiem bad jingshlur khiem 
don kano kano ka jingpynwit ban pynrit mynsiem ia ki. Ki 
kynthei jongngi ki ioh ia ka jinglaitluid ba pura, hynrei ki dei 
pat ban ai ka nuksa kaba bha, ban nym pyndonkam bakla ia 
kane ka jinglaitluid, namar ba ka jinglaituid kam mut satia 
ka jinglaitlan. Ki kynthei ki dei ban ri burom iaiade bad ban 
nym die pathar ia ki jaka puta ne die tad bad shah pyndonkam 
bakla ia ki kyrteng Jong ki ha kano kano ka bynta. Ngi dei 
ban kynmaw ba ka jingim bad ka jingiap jong ka jaidbynriew 
ka shong eh ha ki kynthei. Lada ki kynthei kim mon shuh 
ban pynim ia ka jaidbynriew, kata ka jaidbynriew kan duh 
noh na sla pyrthei. 

Ka jaidbynriew jongngi ka donkam ia ki shynrang bad 
ki rangbah ki ban long ki paid baskhem jong ka Ri, beid 
ki nongker ki nongda, ia ki kynthei ki khynnah. Ki kynthei

ki dei ban don ka jingsngewshngain bad heh mynsiem 
namarba kat shaba ki iaid ki ieng ki don ki rangbah ki ban 
iada ia ki. lada ki kynthei ki tieng bad kim don jingshzmiah 
ha ki shynrang, kata ngim tip aiu kan jia? Ki kynthei 
jongngi ruh iada kim burom bad niew kor ia ki rangbah 
jongngi iano pat kin burom? 

Ha ka jingiadei bad ki khun, ki kmie ki kpa ki dei 
ban pynrung ia ka mynsiem jingkit khiieh ha ki khun 
shynrang bad kynthei. Ka kamram jong ki kmie ki kpa 
kam mut tang ban bsa ban btiah, ban pynkup pynsem bad 
ai jingnang jingstad ia ki, hynrei ban sain dur ia ka longbriew 
membriew, ban long kiba tipbriew tipbiei bad ban tei ia ka 
jingim jong ki khun. Ka jingieit ka long ka jingai bakordor 
tarn jong U Blei ia ngi, hynrei ngim dei bem ieit matlah 
ia la ki khun. Barabor ngi ju iohsngew ba, haba don nrumo 
mano ba iathuh eiei shaphang ki khun ba ki leh kano k£uio 
ka bym dei, ki kmie ki kpa khiem da kylli tohkit ia la ki 
jong ki khun, ngi ju kioi ban ong, ym dei u ne ka khun 


jongngi, ki khun jongngi kini ju leh kunita, kj wan dang 
shai sha ing. Ngi iada, ngi buhrieh, ngi sngcwTcm, sngew 
jah burom bcin ong ba ki khun jongngi kiba leh ia kaei kaei 
ka bymdei. Ngi kloi ban kynnoh ia ki nongiathuh kum kiba 
tomi, kiba nang ban bishar tang ia kiwei bad ki bym shym 
lah ei€i. Lada ngi ki kmie ki kpa ngi iashah bad iada, 
iohlad bad nangshlur pat ki khun. Ka jingieit khun jongngi 
kam dei ban iaiam ia ki sha ka jingjot bad jingpynjot. 
Barabor ngi dei ban da kylli tohkit shuwa ia ki khun la jong. 
Kawei kaba pynjot eh ia ki khun ka long ba, ha ka 
jingkham bit kham biang jongngi ha ka liang ka ioh ka kot, 
ngi kwah ban ai lut ia ki, kat kaba ki pan ne kwah. Teng 
tcng ngi dei ban ong em ia ka jingkwah jong ki. Wat 
tangba kin pciss ia ki examin mh ngi ong ba Iada phi leh

bha, ngin pynthied bad ai kata kata. Ka ktien tip duk tip 
suk, kaba ki kmie ki kpa kiba kham mynshuwa ki iai ban, 
imat kam don shuh ha kiba bun na ngi. 

Ha ka jingiabeh jongngi ia ki kam ki jam, barabor ngi 
ju ong ba ngim don por, ka por ka iaid stet haduh katta 
katta. Bunsien ngi ju iohsngew ruh ba ki khun ki ong ba, 
ki kmie ki kpa kim don por. Kane ka ktah jur ia ki 
khynncih. Ngi dei ban pyrshang ban pynlait por na ka bynta 
la ki jong ki khun, da kaba iasyllok bad ki bad sngap ia 
ki jingud jingeh bad jingdonkam jong ki. Ngi dei ban nang 
ban sneng ban kraw ia ki khun ym da kaba shu hylla 
kyrkait khlem khein ha khmat jongno jongno ruh, ka ing 
ka sem ka dei ban long kaba sngew tynnad bad ba ki khun 
ki dei ban don ka jingkmen, ka jingkwah bad ka jingthrang 
ban poi sha ing, namarba don ka jingieit, ka jingsngewbha 
bzKl ka jingsuk. Lada ki khun kim kwah ban wan kloi sha 
iirg bad kim ieit shuh ia la ka ing, kata ka mut ba ka la 
kut. Ki kmie ki kpa kim dei ban pyni ia kano kano ka 
nuksa kaba sniew ha ki khun. U Abraham Lincoln u ong 
ba "don tang kawei ka rukom ban pynheh pynsan ia u 
khynncih ha ka lynti ba u dei ban iaid, kata ka long ban 



iaid rta ka juh ka iynti iaiade shuwa". Ka nuksa kaba ki 
knVie ki kpa ki im, ka kren kham jam ha ki khun ban ia 
ka kticn. 

(2) Ka shongkha ka shongman : 

Ka shongkha ka shongman ka dei ka bynta ba la buh 
da U Blei, kumta ngi dei ban khein kor ym ban pynhiar 
dor ia ka. Ha kane ka juk mynta la shu ia ieh pathar ia 
ka shongkha ka shongman. Ki khynnah kiba dangrit ka rta,

hapdeng ka jingpule, nadong shadong dang hap peit ki 
kmie ki kpa, khlem kam khlem jam, khlem pynkhreh ia 
lade, khlem pyntip ia ki kmie ki kpa ne ki ba ha ing ha 
sem, ki shu leit ing briew. Lada ym suk ym sain, bym pat 
biang kiei kiei ne na kino kino ki daw, u shynrang u phet 
noh bad ieh khun rei. Kane ka wan rah ia ki ing ki sem 
kiba pait ba pra, ka jingduk jingsahdien, ka jingjynjar bad 
jinghiar dor ka jaidbynriew. Haba ka shongkha ka shongman 
ka bha ka ing ruh ka long kaba bha, bad kaba suk ba sain. 
Dei ki ing ki sem kiba bha kiba pynlong ia ki shnong ki 
thaw kiba bha bad kumjuh ruh kiba kyntiew ia ka Ri bad 
ka jaidbynriew. Kumta, ban tei ne pynpra ia ka longbriew 
manbriew, ka tynrai bah ka dei ka shongkha shongman. 

(3) Ka jingbymtip ia ka tynrai : 

Bun kiba kynnoh ba dei ka dustur jong ngi kaba long 
kumne kaba long kumtai, ngi kynnoh ia ka jingshim jaid bad 
ka jingbym ioh pateng ki khun shynrang kaba pynlong ia ki 
ban khein sting bad kumta ter ter. Ka jingjot bad jinghiar dor 
ka longbriew bad ka imlang sahlang, kam dei na ki riti ki 
dustur, hynrei dei ki jingkylla ba bunjaid kiba wan tyllep 
hapdeng jongngi. Ki jingsniew ba bunjaid, ka jingiatyngkhuh 
bad ki kolshor ba bun jaid ka pynkulmar ia ngi bad ngim lah 
ban sngewthuh shuh. Ngi pyrshang ban bud ia ka jong kiwei 
pat bad klet noh ia la ka jong ka tynrai. Kano kano ka 
jaidbynriew ka bymtip ia la ka jong kam nym neh slem. 
Ngim dei ban long ka jaidbynriew kaba khlem ka tynrai (with- 

<^ 86 ^ _:j=z=^ 


out roots) . Ngi dei ban pynneh ia kiei kiei kiba bha. Kiwei 
pat ki jaidbynriew kin niew kor bad burom iangi lada ngi don 
ka tynrai bad niew kor ia la ka jong.

(4) Ka jingkit khtieh ha ka trei ka ktah : 

Man la ka por ngi iohsngew ia ka jingong ba ki briew 
jong ngi kim don ka jingkit khlieh. Ki trei mon, lehmon, 
khlem don ka jingsngew, Idm tip ia la ka kyrdan haei ki 
don, kim don ka mynsiem trei ne 'work culture'. Mynshuwa, 
ki kmie ki kpa joh^ ngi ki long kiba trei shitom bad kim 
ju kwah ia ka iohei. Ki rep ki riang, ki khaii ki pateng bad 
ki ieit ia kano kano ka kam. Hynrei mynta ngi ia wad iaka 
kam kaba suk. Bun na ngi ngim kweih ban Ieit trei tang 
kham jngai khyndiat na la ing. Ngim ju kwah ban apply 
ia ki kam kiba lait sha kiwei pat ki bynta jong ka Ri India, 
wat ha jylla la jong ruh namar ba jngai ngim treh, ngi salia, 
ngim kwah Ieit, ngim kwah ban pynshitom ia lade. Don ki 
jaid kam kiba ngim kwah ban trei bad kumta ter ter. Ka 
jaidbynriew kaba kiew shaphrang dei ka jaidbynriew kaba 
trei shitom, bad ioh ia ka spah da ka jingtrei shitom. Ngi 
dei ruh ban burom ia kano kano ka kam. 

(5) Ka jingbud tynneng bad kwah ia kiei kiei ki jong 

ka pyrthei (materialism) : 

Ka jingbud tynneng bad ka jingkwah palat ia kiei kiei 
ka long kawei kaba pynsniew ia ka jaidbynriew jongngi. Ka 
don ka jingsngew ba lada kiwei ki ioh balei ngi ngim ioh? 
khamtam eh ki kynthei kim dei ban long ka daw jong ka 
jingkylla jot ki rangbah. Ngi dei ban hun ban im kat kum u 
pud ka jingioh kamai bad jingledi jongngi. Kane ka mynsiem 
rhah ia kiei kiei kiba palat ka bor, bad kwah tynneng palat ki 
kynthei ka lah ban pynjot iaki rangbah, bad ki lah ban leh ia 
kino kino ki kam kiba kim dei bein leh. Ka jingim kaba suk 
tarn ka long ba ngin im kat kum ba kot ka bor, bad ka 
jingioh kamai jongngi. Ka spah bad ka jingioh lut ia kiei kiei

kam nym wem rah satia ia ka jingsuk. 


Sa kawei pat ka jingsniew ka long ka jingkwah spah 
da kaba suk bad ka jingkyrkieh ban ioh spah. Ngi die 
pathar ym tang ki jaka puta, hynrci ngi die bad aiwai wat 
ia la ki kyrteng ruh ha ki kam business, contract etc. Ngi 
kmen ia ka pisa suk bad khlem trei shitom. 

(6) Ka jingbishni jingrhah hapdeng jongngi : 

Ka jingbishni rhah hapdeng jongngi ka long ka 
jinglong kaba sniew, kaba pyntroin ia ka jaidbynriew. U 
thlen ha ka jaidbynriew jongngi dei ka jingbishni. Ngim lah 
ban peit ba kiwei kin kham kiew bad tarn ban ia ngi. 
Kumta kum ka jaidbynriew kaba la shai, ngi dei ban kiar 
na ka jingbishni jingrhah, jinglomi, kyntiap ing bad ngi dei 
ban husiar bad tyngkai bha ia u thylliej bad ka jingrah 
khubor lorni nawei shawei pat, kiba long ki daw jong ka 
jingiamai iania ha bun ki briew bad ka jingpait jong ki ing 
ki sem. 

(7) Ka jinghiar ka akor ka burotn : 

Bun kiba ong ba mynta la hiar ka akor ka burom. Ki 
khynnah kirn nang shuh ban burom ia kiba la san. Ki khynnah 
mynta ki hap ban heh ban san hapdeng shibun bah ki jingkylla 
bad ka jingiaid shaphrang jong ka jingnang ka jingstad. Kumta 
ka dei ka kamram jongngi ban btin lynti ia ki. Haba ngi kren 
shaphang ka jinghiar dor ka akor ka burom ha kine ki por 
mynta, ngi dei ruh ban kylli iaiade, la ngi la leh ne em ia kaei 
kaba ngi dei ban leh? La ngi la long ne cm ka nuksa jong 
kiba dang khie dang san? La ngi long ne em ki longkmie ki 
longkpa, ki kni ha kaba ngin ioh ia ka burom bad ka 
jingshynrain jong ki? Namar kane ka dei ka juk jong ka jingstad,

bad ki khynnah bad samia ki peit bha ia kaei kaba leh ym ia 
kaei kaba ngi ia lap. 

(8) Sa kiwei kiwei pat ki jingsniew: 

Sa kiwei kiwei pat ki jingsniew ha ka imlang sahlang 
ki long ka jingdih buaid, ki drugs, khalai bad ki films bad 


ki kot kiba sniew kiba pynjot bad pynhiar dor ia ka 
longbriew man briew bad ia ka Ri bad ka jaidbynriew. 

(9) Ka jingpynkut : 

Ba ka jaidbynrievv jongngi kan roi bad kiew shaphrang, 
ka dci ban kiew lut ha kut ki liang baroh, ha ka jingnang 
ka jingstad kat kum Ki sap ki phong kiba U Blei u la ai 
ha ngi. Ngi dci ban kiew ha ka ioh ka kot bad ban bat 
ia ka khaii ka pateng bad ka rep ka riang. Ngi dei ban 
burom ia baroh ki jaid kam, bad ban ym Ichrain ban trci 
ia kano kano ka kam. Ngi dei ban long ka jaidbynriew 
kaba ieng ha la ki jong ki kjat. Ngi dei ban long kiba trei 
shitom bad ban kamai bad iohspah da ka jingtrei shitom, 
ym ban kmcn ia ka iohei, bam sap bad pisa suk. Ngi dei 
ban long kynrad ha la ka Ri la jong, ngim dei ban die 
pathar ia ki jaka puta, bad ban ai wai ia ki kyrteng lajong 
ha kano kano ka bynta. Ngi dei ban kiar na kiwei kiwei 
ki jingsniew kum ki drugs, ka jingdih buaid, ka khalai bad 
ki jingmlien ba sniew. Ngi dei ban nang ban pyndonkam 
ia ki jingstad kiba ka juk mynta ka ai kum ka T.V. Video 
etc. Ngim dei ban long ka jaidbynriew kaba la kiew ha ka 
jingnang jingstad, bad hiar pat ha ka akor ka burexn, ka 
longbriew. Khadduh eh, kumba don ka jingong, lada ngi 
kwah ban pynkylla ia ka pyrthei, ngi dei ban sdang shuwa 
na lade.

Ki Seng bhalang ha kaba 
kyntiew ia ka imlang sahlang 


Ki Seng bhaleuig, ki dei ki scng trei mon sngevt/bha 
bad ki dei ki seng ki bym dei ki jong ka Sorkar. Ki Seng 
bhalang ki don ha baroh ki bynta jong ka pyrthei bad 
kumjuh ha Ri India bad ka jylla Meghalaya jong ngi nih. 
la ki Seng bhalang la ju tip mh, ha ka ktien Phareng, kum 
ki non-government organisations, ne haba shu ong lyngkot 
ki NGO's. Ki don bun jaid ki Seng bhalang, kat kum ki 
kynhun, ka bynta bad ka rukom trei kam jong ki. Kum 
ka nuksa ki don ki Seng kynthei, ha kaba ki dkhot ki dei 
ki samla bad samla pule. Ki don ruh ki seng thoh seng 
pule, ha kaba ki dkhot ki dei ki nongthoh kot, bad ki briew 
kiba sngewtynnad ban thoh bad ban pynphriang ia ka thoh 
ka pule. Nalor nangta, don ruh sa ki seng kiba iadei bad 
ki kam ba ngi trei, ki seng kiba kwcdi ban pynneh pynsah 
ia ka kolshor jong ka jaidbynriew, ka niam ka rukom, ka 
ru/ai ka siaw, ka put ka tern, ka shad ka kmen bad ka 
rukom ialeh kai. Ki don ruh ki seng kiba iadei bad ka khaii 
ka pateng ne bad ki niam kiba pher, bad sa kiwei kiwei 
de kiba ngim lah ban niew kyrteng lut. Kine ki seng trei 
mon sngewbha, ki don ka bynta kaba khraw tarn, ha kaba 
tei bad kyntiew ia ka imlang sahlang jong kano kano ka 
jaidbynriew. Ki Seng bhalang ki wanrah ia ka jingieit, 
jingiatylli, ha ki dkhot kiba don ha kine ki seng. Ki teh song 
ia ki kynhun, ki wanrah ia ka mynsiem trei kam lang, ban 
jop ia ka thong, bad ka mynsiem ban ialeh bad kiwei pat. 

Ki daw ba kongsan jong ka jingmih ki seng bhalang 
ka long ba u briew u dei u 'social being', kumba ong ki

Phareng, u donkam ia ka imlang sahlang, trei lang bad um 
lah satia ban im marwei. Kine ki seng ki ai ka lad ban 
ia trei lang. Ki seng bhalang ki mih ruh na ka jingbymhun 


bad suk ne ka jingsngew pyrshah ia kiei kiei kiba don ha 
ka imiang sahlang. Ki Seng bhalang ki long Rih ki "Pressure 
Group” kiba iai kyntu ne dawa ia ki bor, ba kin leh ne 
pyntreikam ia kiei kiei ki jingthmu, bad jingdawa jong ki. 
^roh ki seng bhalang ki dei ban don ka jingthmu bad ka 
nongrim kaba skhem. Ki dei ban don ki mat trei kam bad 
ki prokram trei kam. Ki dei beun don ruh ki Riti pyniaid 
kam (Constitution) bad ki nong kitkam. Bun na ki seng 
bhalang kiba la register kat kum ka society Registration 
Act. Ka jingmyntoi ban register ia ki seng ka long ba la 
ithuh ia ki da ka sorkar bad ki lah ban ioh ruh ki jingiarap 
ha bun ki liang na ka sorkar bad kiwei de ki lad jingiarap. 
Ka jingkhlain ne ka jingtlot jong kano kano ka seng bhalang 
ka shong eh ha ki nongkitkam jong ka. Ki nong kitkam 
ki dei ban long kiba trei shitom bad len lade bad kiba don 
ruh ka por ban trei. Ha ka jylla Meghalaya jongngi, ngi iohi 
ba la mih bun tylli ki seng bhalang, khamtam ha kine ki 
arphew snem ba la dcp. Bauroh ki seng pule ne kino kino 
ki seng, ki pyrshang ban trei ha la ki jong ki jong ki bynta 
kat kaba sngew lah, khnang ban kyntiew, ban pynbha bad 
pynkylla ia ka imiang sah lang. 

Haba ngi peit bad bishar ia ki Seng bhalang, ngi 
shem ba ki la pyrshang ban leh ia kiei kiei kiba bha,, na 
ka bynta ka imiang sahlang, ka ioh ka kot, ka jingnang ka 
jingstad, ka koit ka khiah, ka kam ka jam, bad kiwei kiwei 
de. Ki Seng kynthei ki don ha man la ki dong bad ki 
shnong. Ki kynthei wat la ki bunkam katno katno ruh, ki 
pyrshang bad trei shitom tyngeh, khamtam ban rat 
diengkhong ia ki . jingsniew kiba don ha ka imiang ka

sahlang, kum ka kyiad, khalai bad drugs, kiba pyntroin ia 
ka jaidbynriew. Ki trei ruh ban kyntiew ia ka thoh ka pule, 
da kaba plie ia ki skul, bad ai jinghikai ia kiba la san, bad 
kito kiba la iehnoh ia Id jingpule. Ki Seng kynthei ki ju ai 
jinghikai ruh ban suh ban thain,ban shet, kumno ban sumar 
ia Id kmie bad ki khyllung. Ki don ki Seng kynthei kiba 
plie ia ki Creche ne ki jaka bem buh ia ki khun, khnang 


ban iarap ia ki kmie kiba leit trei. Ki Seng kynthei ki ju 
pynlong ruh ia ki Prokram thung dieng ban iarap ban 
pynneh pynsah ia ka mariang. Ki Seng kynthei ki ju 
pyrshah ruh man ka por ia ka jingkiew palat ki dor ki mur, 
ka jingbymbha bad ioh slem ia u khaw na ki dukan ration, 
bad kiwei kiwei de ki kam babha, kiba ngim lah ban niew 
lut. Ki Seng Sarnia, ruh ki leh la ka bynta, kumno ban 
kyntiew ia ka jingnang ka jingstad, ki lad iohkam iohjam. 
Ki ju kyntu bad pynshlur ia ki samla, ban pyndonkam ia 
ki sap ki phong ba ki don, da kaba pynlong ia ki jingialehkai 
Competition, kum ki Sports, debate, essay, cultural pro- 
gramme bad kiwei kiwei de. Ki ju long nih ki nongpeitngor 
ia ka imiang sahlang, ki jingsniew ba bun jaid kum ka 
kyiad, khalai, bad kiwei kiwei de. Ki don ruh ki Seng kiba 
trei shitom ban kyntiew ia ka thoh ka pule, ka ktien ka 
thylliej. Ki don ki Seng kiba kwah ban pynneh pynsah ia 
ka kolshor jong ka jaidbynriew, kum ka shad ka kmen, ka 
rwai ka siaw bad ka put ka tern, khnang ban pynphuh 
pynphieng ia kiei kiei kiba ka jaidbynriew ka don. 

Ka jingroi jingpar jong ka Ri kam shong eh tang ha 
ka Sorkar. Ka donkam ka jingiatrei lang hapdeng ka 
Sorkar, ki paidbah bad ki Seng bhalang. Kumba paw 
mynta, ki seng bhalang khamtam eh ki long ka kti kamon 
ka kti kadiang jong ka Sorkar bad jong ki paidbah. Kumta 
ki Seng bhalang ki dei ban trei bha, ban ioh ia ka jingkyrshan

bad ka jingshaniah jong u paidbah, khnemg ban lah ban 
trei bha, ban ioh ia ka jingkyrshan bad ka jingshaniah jong 
u paidbah, khnang ban lah ban wanrah shibun ki jingkylla, 
bad jingbha ha ka imiang sahlang. Dei ban weng noh ia 
ki jingsniew kiba bun ha ka imjang sahlang bad ka jingduk 
jingkyrduh bad ka ^gbym nangthoh nangpule khamtam ha 
ki kam nangroi nang par, bad nang kiew shaphrang ha 
baroh ki Hang. 


IV 

KA JINGNANG JINGSTAD BAD 
KA THOH KA PULE 

Ka jingiaid shai^FEing jong ka Adult Education 
ne ka jingai jinghikai ia kiba la san ha 
Meghalaya (1978-1991) 


Ka jingnang ka jingstad, ka jingnangthoh nang pule 
ka bng kaba donkam shibun eh, bad kam kut noh, tang 
ha ka jingleit skul, college ne university, hynrei, ka iaid 
ryngkat lang, bad shi lynter ka jingim jong u briew, bad ki 
^ngkylla kiba bun ha ka imking sah lang. Wat la ka long 
kaba donkam, hynrei, da ki million ki briew ha ka Ri India 
jongngi, khamtam ha ki nongkyndong, ki bym nang thoh 
nang pule. Ha ka snem 1951, ki don kumba 300 million 
ngut, bad ha ka snem 1981, ki poi sha ka 437 million 
ngut. Ki bym nang thoh nang pule, hapoh ka rta 15 haduh 
35 snem, ki don 91 million ngut ha ka snem 1951, bad 
110 million ngut ha ka snem 1981. Ha ka snem 1991, 
ka jingkiew ka long sha ka 116 million ngut. Kumta, ka 
^gbymnang thoh nang pule jong ki briew, hapoh ka rta 
15 haduh 35 snem, - ka la nang kiew nang kiew. Ki daw

ba kongsan jong kane ka jingsahdien ki long, kum ka 
nuksa, ka jingduk jingkyrduh, ka jingjngai jong ki skul ha 
iKMnglonndong, ka rukom trei jong ki briew, ki bynnud ban 
ieh ia la ki kam, bem leit skul. Don ruh shibun, kiba iehnoh 
ia ki jingpule na bun ki daw, kiba, ngi khot ha ka ktien 
Pheureng ki 'drop outs'. 

Kumta, ban rat dyngkhong ia ka jingbymnangthoh 
nangpule, khamtam hapdeng kiba la san, la sdang ia ka 


National Adult Education Programme, nc ka jingai ^nghikai 
ia kiba la san, ha ka 2 tarik u Risaw 1978, kaba hap ruh 
ha ka sngikha jong u Mahatma Gandhi, u kpa j(xig ka 
jaidbynriew, u ba ju kwah bad thrang barabor, ban pynduh 
pyndam ia ka jingshah banbein, ka jingbieit bad jingstdi 
dien, bad ban ieng, ha la Id jong id kjat. Ka jingthmu ba 
kongsein jong ka National Adult Education Programme ka 
long ban weng noh ia ka jingbymnangthoh nang pule bad, 
ban ai jinghikai ia kite kiba la san, hapoh ka rta 15 haduh 
35 snem. Ka long kaba donkam shibun beui ai jinghikai ia 
kiba la san hapoh kane ka rta, namar, dei ha kane ka por 
baSki la nang bad tbit ban trei shibun Id kam, bad ka dei 
ka por, ha kaba, bun na Id Id la shongkurim. Ka jingthmu 
jong kane ka programme, kam kut tang ha kaba hikai thoh 
bad pule, hynrei ha ka juh ka por ban hikai ia ki kam ki 
jam bad ki jingtbit jong ki briew, ban wan rah ka ^gkyila 
ha ka imiang sahlang, ka trei ka ktah, ka ioh ka kot, 
khnang ba u briew, u lah ban im kat kum ka ^gkylla jong 
ka por. Ka jingnang thoh nangpule, ka jingnang ka jingstad, 
ka long kaba donkam ia baroh, wat ia kiba la heh la san, 
na ka bynta ka jingroi ka jingpar bad jin^kiew shaphrting 
jong ka Ri, khamtam, ka Ri India jongngi kaba don ka 
jingsynshar paidbah, bad ki briew Id dei ban tip ia kaei 
kaba ki leh.

Haba, ngi phai sha ka jylla Meghalaya jongngi, ngi 
shem na ki jinglum jingtip ba ka percentage jong ka 
jingnangthbh nang pule, ka long 30.08% ha ka snem 
1981, bad 39% ha ka snem 1991, bad lada iehnoh ia kito 
kiba hapoh ka 6 snem ka rta, kan poi kumba 48.26%. 
Kane ruh, ka dang duna shibun eh, haba ia nujor bad ka 
percentage jong ka Ri India betfoh fcawei kaba long 52. 1 1%. 
Ha ka jylla Meghalaya, ka jingbymnang thoh nang pule, 
hapdeng kito kiba hapoh 15 haduh 35 snem ka rta, ha 
ka snem 1991, ka long kumba 2 lak 62 hajar ngut. 


Wat lada la sdang ia ka National Adult Education 
Programme ha ka Ri, ha ka snem 1978, hynrei, la sdang 
pyntrei kam ia ka, ha ka jylla Meghalaya ha ka snem 
1980-81, namar ki snem 1978-79, ki long ki snem ka 
jingpynkhreh ia kane ka programme. Ha shuwa ban pyntrei 
kam ia ka National Adult Education Programme ha ka jylla 
ia ka Adult Education, ne ka jingai jinghikai ia kiba la san, 
la pyntrei kam lyngba ka Social Education Wing, Jong ka 
Education Department. Ka Social Education ka aiti hadien, 
ha ki Block Development Officer bad ki field staff kiba 
kynthup ia ki social Education organiser, bad Lady Social 
Education organisers. Hapoh ki District level, ki don amgut 
ki District social Education officer, u wei ha Shillong, ban 
peit ia ka East, West Khasi bad Jaintia Hills, bad, u wei 
pat, ha Tura ban peit ia ka East bad West Garo Hills. 
La iarap ia ki ha ka ‘state level' da u Deputy Director. 
Hadien, la kham pynbha ia ka rukom trei kam. Ha ka 
'state level' la don u Deputy Director bad Assistant Director, 
ban peit halor ka Adult Education. Ha baroh 5 tylli ki 
District jong ka jylla, don ki E)istrict Adult/Social officer. Ha 
ka 'field level' pat, don sa ki social education organisers bad 
ki Lady social education organisers, kiba peit ba khmih ia

ki jaka ai jinghikai, ne lympung jingtip na ka bynta kiba 
la san. 

Ki artylli ki rukom pyntrei kam hapoh ka National 
Adult Education Programme, ki long, ka Rural Functional 
Literacy Programme (RFLP), kaba ioh ka jingiarap na ka 
Sorkar kmie, bad ka stale Adult Education Programme 
(SAEP) kaba dei jong ka jylla. Baroh ki lympung ai jinghikai 
ki dei lang na ka bynta ki kynthra bad shynrang. Ha ka 
snem 1984, baroh ki lympung ai jinghikai ia kiba la san, 
kiba kynthup lang ia ki Rural Functional Literary Programme 
(RFLP), bad ki state Adult Education Programme (SAEP), 
ki don 1200 tylli. Ha ka snem 1986-87, ki la don haduh 
1400 tylli, bad ha ka snem 1988-89 ki don haduh 1450 


tylli. Naduh ka snem 1981-82, haduh 1985-86, na ka 
thong kaba la buh, ban ai jinghikai ia ki 1,77000 ngut, 
1,33,148 ngut, la lah ban pynrung ha kine ki jaka ai 
jinghikai. Na kine, 81,356 ngut dei ki shynrang bad 51,792 
ngut ki kynthei, bad ka percentage ka long 91% ki shynrang, 
bad 58% ki kynthei. Kiba sah haduh kaba kut ka jinghikai, 
ki long 62,196 ngut ki shynrang, bad, 39,655 ngut ki 
kynthei, Na kine, tang 29,483 ngut ki shynrang bad 18,060 
ngut ki kynthei kiba la lah ban ong, ba ki la nangthoh nang 
pule. Ka percentage ka long 47.40% ia ki shynrang, bad 
45.54% ia ki kynthei, bad kyllum lang ka long 46.68%. Ha 
ka snem 1988-89, ki lympung ai jinghikai, ki don baroh 
1450 tylli. Ka thong ban ai jinghikai ka long, na ka bynta 
36,250 ngut, bad kynthei bad shynrang, bad kiba la lah 
ban ai jinghikai, ki long 34,466 ngut. Napdeng kine, 
16,673 ngut kiba la lah ban ong, ba ki la nangthoh nang 
pule. Ha ka snem 1989-90, la don 1500 tylli ki jaka ai 
jinghikai, ka thong ban ai jinghikai ka long na ka bynta 
37,500 ngut kiba la ioh jinghikai ki long 35,919 ngut, bad

kiba la lah ban ong ba ki la nangthoh nang pule, ki long 
16,463 ngut. Ha ka snem 1990-91, ki don 1500 tylli ki 
jaka ai jinghikai, 28,460 ngut ki la ioh jinghikai bad 40% 
la lah ban pynnang thoh nang pule. 

Kumta, ka la trei kam, ka Adult Education, ne ka 
jingai jinghikai ia kiba la san ha Meghalaya, la ngi lah ban 
ong, ba ym pat shym jop lut ia ka thong bad dang bun 
ki jingduna. Ka Department ka la pynmih bun ki kot pule, 
kot jingkhein, bad kiwei kiwei de, na ka bynta ki nonghikai 
bad ki nongshah hikai. La ai ruh ki kynja jinghikai ne 
’training’ ia ki nonghikai. 

Ka National Policy on Education 1986, ka wan rah 
ia ka Mass Programme for Functional Literacy (MPFL^, ban 
pyntrei kam kham bha ia ka National Programme Adult 
Education . la ka programme thymmai la khot ka National 


Utcraq/ Mission, bad la sdang ha ka sncm 1988. Kane ka 
dei ka programme jong ki paidbah, ha kaba ki samla pule, 
ki nonghikai, ki seng bhalang bad kiwei kiwei ruh, ki lah 
ban iatrei bad ai kti lang. Ka jingfhmu ba kyrpang jong ka 
national Literacy Mission ka long, ban jop ia ka thong ban 
pynnang thoh nangpule ia ki 80 million ngut ki briew jong 
ka Ri India, kiba hapoh ka rta 15 haduh 35 snem, kat 
ha kaba kut jong ka snem 1995. Ha kajuh ka por, ban 
weng noh ia ka jingbymnang thoh nang pule na ka Ri, da 
kaba pyndonkam lut ia ki buit ki bor bad ki buit stad jong 
ka science bad Technology, kiba don ha ka Ri. 

Kumba la long mynshuwa, ba ka jinghikai ia kiba la 
san, kam mut tang ban hikai thoh ne pule, hynrei ban hikai 
ia ki kam ki jam, ki jingtbit jong ki briew. Khnang ban 
iarap ia kito kiba la namgthoh nangpule, bein nym klet ia

kaei kaba ki la pule, la sdang 225 ki jaka ai jinghikai bad 
pule kot, kaba ki khot ki Jana Shikseui Nilayam, kat kum 
ka Programme jong ka National Literacy Mission. Kumba 
la kren haneng ki bym nang thoh nang pule ha ka jylta 
Meghalaya kiba hapoh ka rta 15-35, ki don kumba 2 lak 
62 hajar ngut. Na kine, kumba 2 lak ki don ha ki 
nongkyndong, bad kumba 62 hajar ngut ki don ha sor. La 
thmu ban plie kumba 8000 tylli ki jaka ai jinghikai na ka 
bynta kine ki 2 lak ngut ha ki nongkyndong. Ha ka jylla 
Meghalaya, don shibun ki nongrit nongkyndong, hynrei kim 
don shi jaka, ki saphret shane shatai. Kumta, ka long kaba 
eh ia ki khynnah skul bad samla pule ne kino kino ki seng 
Bhalang ban leit ai jinghikai sha ki nongkyndong, ki lah ban 
leh tang ha sor. Sha ki nongkyndong, lah ban ai jinghikai 
tang ha ki jaka jinghikai ne ki lympung jingtip. Ka Sorkar 
jylla ka ai jingiarap pisa ruh ia ka North Eastern Hill 
University, lyngba ka Department of Adult and Continuing 
Education, ban weng noh ia ka jingbymnang thoh nang 
pule ha ka nongbah Shillong. 


Kumta ngi shem ba ka National Literacy Mission ka 
dei ka programme kaba donkam shibun eh na ka bynta ka 
Ri, na kata ka daw, la pyntrei kam ia ka ha ka dur jpng 
ka Mission. La thmu bctn pyndonkam lut ia ki buit ki bor 
baroh, khnang ban pynduh pyndam ia ka jingbymnang thoh 
nang pule khamtam hapdeng Idba la san hapoh 15 haduh 
35 snem ka rta. Kano kano ka Ri, kan nym lah satia ban 
kiew shaphrang, lada ki briew kim nang thoh nangpule. 
Kumta, la sngew donkam, ba ka sorkar ka dei ban wan 
rah shibun Id programme, ban pynmih shibun ki kot ai 
jingiarap ia ki nonghikai, bad kumjuh ruh ia ki nongshah 
hikai. Donkam ruh ki training kiba kham bha ia ki nonghikai 
bad nongiarap. Khatduh eh, ym dei tang ka Central ne 
state Government kiba lah ban leh, hynrei ryngkat lang,

bad ka jingaikti lem jong ki paidbah, ban ialeh ban jop ia 
kane ka thong, ban weng noh ia ka jingbymnang thoh 
nangpule, khamtam hapdeng kiba la san, na ka jylla 
Meghalaya, bad ka Ri India hi baroh kawei. 




KI SAMLA BAD KA JINGNANG JINGSTAD 


Ka jingnang ka jingstad ka long kaba kordor haduh 
katta katta, ka dei mh ka atiar kaba khlain tarn, ban i'ada 
na kino kino ki jingeh bad jingshah ban bein ne leh bein. 
U Chanakya uwei na ki 'riewstad u ong, "ka jingnang ka 
jingstad ka dei ka spah bah kaba yrn don ba lah ban tuh 
noh, kan iai don hi bad ngi wat laJa ngi ai lem ia khvei". 
Haba kylli ia uwei pat u 'riewstad, u Aristotle, "haduh katno 
u briew uba la nang uba la stad u kham khraw ban i'a 
uto u bym nang u bym stad, u jubab, katba long uba im 
ia uba Tap". Ngi im ha kane ka juk, ha kaba ka jingnang 
ka jingstad ha kut ki liang ka la kiew lut, bad teng teng, 
ngim lah shuh ban sngewthuh. Mynta ka dei ka por ha 
kaba ngi ialeh tang da ka jabieng, ka jingstad bad ka 
jingshem phang. Kano kano ka Ri bad ka jaidbynriew kan 
nym lah satia ban kiew shaphrang, lada ngim khein kor la 
ka jingnang ka jingstad, bad pyntbit i'a lade ha bun ki liang, 
kat kum ki sap ki phong kiba ngi don. Ka Sorkar ka don 
ka jingkit khlieh kaba khia shibun eh, ha kaba kyntiew i'a 
ka thoh ka pule, ka jingnang ka jingstad, ka dei ka 
kamram jong ka Sorkar ban peit bad pynkhlain ia ka tnat 
trei kam ai jingnang jingstad, khnang ba ka jylla kan kiew 
shaphrang. 

Haba ngi phai sha ka por jinglong samla, ngi shem

ba ka long ka por kaba sngewtynnad tarn, ka por jong ki 
jingsngewbha bad jingkmen, hynrei ka long ruh ka por jong 
ki jingi'aleh, kaba dap da ki jingeh ba bun jaid kiba ki samla 
ki hap betn kynduh. Dei ruh ha kane ka por, ba ki samla 
ki saihdur i'a ka jingim bad ki i'oh i'a ka jingnang ka 
jingstad. Lada ki im 'la ka jingim kaba bha, khlem pynsyrwa 
ei i'a la ka por ha ki kam kai kam thala, ki samla ki lah 


ban tei ia ka lawei ba phymai. kat kum ka ^gthmu jof^ 
bun ki samla pule. Hynrei lada ki bakla bad lap-miet ha 
ki kam ki bym ai ^ngmyntoi, kin sa babe hacfien. Ka jingai 
ka bakhraw, bad ka spah bah, ba ki kmie ki kpa kiba kot 
bor, ki ai ha ki khun ka dei ka jingnang ka jingstzKl. Bun 
ki khynnah bad samla kiba duk kiba wan na nongiqmdong,

Ki ling ki sem ki don ka bynta kaba khraw tam ha 
ka imiang sahlang. Dei ka ing ka sem kaba sain dur ia ka

la rymF)ei. Dei ka jingkordor jong ki ing ki sem ha bun ki

kyrpang jong ki ing ki sem. Dei ki ing ki sem kiba skhem 
bad kiba khlain kiba kyrshan ia ki Balang, ka Ri bad ka

ki khun naduh dangrit. Kumta ia ki ing ki sem kristan dei 
ban tei ha ka nongrim jong ka jingieid, ka jingshaniah, ka

nuksa kaba bha ia la ki jong ki khun. Dei ki kmie ki kpa 
kiba hikai ia ki khun naduh dangrit kumno ban tip ia u

niam. Ki hikai ruh ia ka akor ka burom ka longbriew, ban 
pule kot, ban minot ban khuid ban suba bad kumta ter ter. 
Dei ki ing ki sem kristan kiba pynlong ia ki Balang kiba 
bha, ki shnong ki thaw kiba bha bad ka Ri bad ka 
jaidbynriew kaba bha. 

Dang shen ha kaba plie ia ka jingrakhe ia u snem 
1994, kum u snem jong ki ing ki sem shityllup ka pyrthei,

Ka Jingpynkut 

Ha kine ki por mynta, baroh ngi ia ud bym don por. 
ka por ka iaid stet, ka jingstad pyrthei, khamtam ha ka

liang ka Science bad Technology ka la kiew bad iaid stet 
haduh katta katta. Baroh ngi ia beh ia kiei kiei ki jong ka

kyiirci h.d ka iinlang sahlang, ka thma.ka jingbymiasngewthuh 
jingmul. ka jinqbishni, ka jingpihuin, ka jingkwah bor bad

sem ngi bunkam, ba hap pynbiang ia ki khun ki kti, ym 

don por ba trci tam ban kham ioh pisa ha kane ka tan 

pyrthei. Ki bun kam khreh kot, leh homework, leit tuition, 
ki jingpule kiba eh suda, mynta ba dei ka por jong ki 
jingialeh (Age of competition) ha kaba tang uta bit tam u 
lah ban im bad tur shaphrang. Kumta ka pyrthei mynta ka 
dap da ki jingeh suda. Hynrei wat la ngi duwai ha la 
marwei marwei, ngi dei ban pyrshang ban pyllait por ban 
duwai lang , namar ka jingduwai ka long ka bor ban pyllait 
ia ngi na bun ki jingeh bad pynkhlain ia ki ing ki sem jong 
ngi. Ngi dei ban kynmaw ba ka ing ka sem kaba duwai 

lang ka don n/ngkat lang bad ngin duwai da kaba ngeit, 

ka jingsngewrit ka jingshitrhem, ka jingiaineh bad jingks^rmen.

dei ban leh. Kum ka jaidbynriew ngi dei ban sngewnguh ia ki 
Mishonari na shiliang duriaw kiba la wan ban sengnongrim ia 
ka thoh ka pule, ban ai ruh ia ki dak thoh (Script). Ka Balang 
Katholik ha kine ki shispah snem tarn, ka la noh synniang 
radbah ha kaba pynphriang ia ka thoh ka pule da kaba seng 
ia ki skul rit, ki High School, Ki College, Ki Skul Trei, Ki 
Hospital, Ki Dispensary na ka bynta ka jingkoit jingkhiah, 
bad sa kiwei kiwei de ki lad ai jinghikai. Kumta, ki briew ki 
la kiew ha ki kyrdan bapher jong ka thoh ka pule bad ka 
longbriew manbriew. 

Ha ki por mynshuwa ki Khasi kim don jingthoh 
jingtar la ka jong. Hynrei wat lada kim nang ban thoh ban 
pule, hynrei ki long ka jaidbynriew kaba don la ka jong ka 
jingstad bad jingshemphang. Ki don la ka jong ka sain 
pyrkhat, ka synshar khadar, ka sain hima sima, ka niam 
ka rukom, ka shad ka kmen, ka put ka tern, ka ktien ka 
thylliej, ka riam ka beit bad la ki jong ki khanatang, ki 
puriskam bad purinam. La iathuh pateng la pateng ba ka 
tynrai jingthoh ne dak thoh Khasi ka la duh noh ha ka 


hima ba ia rim tarn ka hima Madur Maskut. Ka don nih 
ha kawei ka khana kaba iathuh ba ka jin9thoh tynrai ka 
la jah noh ha ka jingshlei urn. Kumta, haduh ka shiteng 
jong ka spah snem ba la leit, ki Syiem ki ju pyndonkeun 
da ki dak thoh jong ki paramarjan kum ka Devanagiri, 
Assamese, Bengalee, bad ruh ka Persian. Ka jingiadei 
hapdeng ki nongsynshar bad ki khap pud thor, ka la

pynlong ia ki ban pyndonkam ia kitei ki jaid ktien. Dei tad 
haduh ka snem 1841, haba la mih pli da ki dak thoh 
Roman ba ka jingpyndonkam ia kitei ki jaid ktien thoh ka 
la kut noh. 

Ka jingiatyngkhuh nyngkong jong ki Khasi bad ki 
Phareng ka la long ha ka 1765, ha kaba ka East India 
Company ka la ioh ia ka "Dewani" na ki Moghul. Da kane 
ki phareng ki la ioh ia ka Sylhet bad ki la ia kynduh bad 
ki Khasi, bad ki la sngewthuh ia ka jingdonkam ka khaii 
pateng bad ki, khamtam eh ia u Mawshun u ba long u 
babha tarn. Shibun ki jingkylla ki ia wctpf ha kane ka Ri 
jongngi. Ha u snem 1833, la pyniasoh (Annexed) ia ka 
Khasi Hills, bad ka jingsynshar ki phareng hadien ka thma 
hapdeng ki Khasi bad ki Phareng (1829-1833) hapoh ka 
jingialam jong u Tirot sing, u syiem jong ka Hima Nongkhlaw. 
Ha u snem 1835, ki phareng ki la pyniasoh (Annexed) ia 
ka Hima Jaintia. Nongkong ki la kwah ban pyniasoh tang 
ia ki bynta ri thor jong ka hima, bad ki la shah ia u Syiem 
Rajendra Sing ban synshar tang ia ki bynta Ri-lum jong ka 
Hima. Hynrei u Syiem um kw/ah ban synshar tang ia Id 
bynta ri-lum, kumta u la ieh lang noh bad la pyniasoh ia 
ka Hima Jaintia bad ki Phareng. 

Ha u snem 1813, ka Parliament jong ka Ri Bilat ka ia 
weng noh ia baroh ki jingkhang jingpynwit (Restrictions) halor 
ka jingtrei kam ki Mishonari ha India. Da ka clause XXXIl 
jong ka Charter Act 1813, la ai ka jinglaitluid ban pynphriang 
ia ka Niam Khristan. Kumta la sdang ka jingpynphriang ia 



ka niam Khristan ha India bad shaduh ki bynta ba shatei lam 
mihngi jong ka Ri India (N.E.India). U David Scott uba long u 
Chief Commissioner jong ka Assam, u la thoh shitfii sha ka

sorkar (British Government) ha u bnai April, 27, 1825 ha 
kaba u la kyrpad ban sheih ia ki Mishonari ban sdang ki kam 
babha hapdeng ki riewlum jong ka Assam. U sngew ba ym 
don kano kano ka ban kham bha ban ia ka jingpynphriang ia 
ka Gosp>eI sha kine ki thain. U David Scott, lehse u don ka 
jingpyrkhat ba kane ka dei ka lad ban pynjem bad pynjaijai 
ia ki 'riev^um kiba ehrang. U da ong, ba lada ka sorkar kam 
lah ban ai jingiarap, ma u hi un iarap pisa ia ki Mishoneri. 
Ka sorkar ka la ai jingbit ia u David Scott ban ia kren bad 
kino kino ki Mishonari. Hynrei ha kajuh ka por ki la ai jingbit 
ban khot ia ki Mishonari ban shim ia kaei kaba ki khot 
"Mission of Civilization" ne ka Mishon ai jingshai ia ki riewlum 
kiba dang sah ha ka jingdum. Kane ka dei ka jingthmu jong 
ka sorkar ne ka "Official View". Hynrei ki Mishonari ki lah 
ban nym don kum kata ka jingthmu, hynrei ban pynphriang 
ia ka Gospel bad ka khubor babha jong U Jisu Khrist sha ki 
bynta ba shatei lam mihngi jong ka Ri India. 

La sdang ia ka history jong ka thoh ka pule da ka 
Serampore Baptist Mission. U Nongprat lynti ba nyngkong 
dei u William Carey, u ba la phah ia u Krishna Chandra 
Pal uba la shah pynbaptis nyngkong eh ha u. U Krishna 
Chandra Pal u la mih na Serampore bad poi ha Sylhet ha 
ka snem 1813. U la ioh ban pynbaptis hynniew ngut ki 
briew, bad napdeng kine argut ki dei ki Kheisi, U Duwam 
bad ka Anna. Ha ka snem 1824, la pynwan sha ka ktien 
Khasi ia ka New Testament ha ka rukom kren Shelia da 
ki dak Bengalee, la pynmih ruh katto katne ki kot. Ha ka 
snem 1832, u William Carey u la phah ia u Alexandar 
B.Lish sha Sohra. Une u mishonari u la pynkylla sha ka 
Khasi ia ka Gospel u St. Mathew bad kiwei kiwei de ki 
bynta jong ka New Testament ha ki dak thoh Bengalee. 
U la pUe lai tylli ki skul bad u la mih noh na Sohra ha u

sncm 1838.

Welsh Presbyterian Mission. Kine ki Mishonari ki la wan 
rah shibun ki jingkylla ha ka thoh ka tar. Ki la sdang ban

Ki Mishonari Katholik ki la long kiba nyngkong ban 
iuh kjat ha ki bynta ba shatei lam mihngi. Hynrei ka shim 
por bun snem hadien ka jingwan jong ki Welsh Presbyterian 
Mission, ban wan poi sha kine ki lum jong ngi. Ha u snem 
1626, u Phadar Cacella bad u Brodar Cabral kiba na

Cochin Province ki la iuh kjat ha Assam. Ka ^ngdimu jong

Gauhati ha ka 26 tarik Nailur 1626 bad ki la leit sha 
Cooch Bihar. 

U Phadar Freuicis Laynez u jong ki Jesuit bad u ba 
dei na Portugal, hadien ba u la long Bishop jong ka 
Mylapore, u la wan peit ia ka Bengal, Chandemagorc, 
Chittagong bad Dacca. U la mih na Dacca ha ka snem 
1714, bad u la poi ha Rangamati kaba ha Cooch Bihar 
ha kaba don katto katne ki katholik. Don nih ki Katholik 
ha Bandashill hajan Badarpur (Cachar) kiba la ngeit ba Id 
la wan ha kaba sdang jong ka spah snem ba khatphra, bad

ki hiar pateng na ki Portuguese kiba wan bad ki Mughuls.

hadien na Dacca. Kine ki katholik ki dang don haduh 
mynta. Don ruh ki Katholik ha Mariamnagar ha ^artah, 
ym da don jingtip eh shaphang jong ki.

Ka Balang Katholik ka la trei ruh na ka bynta ban 
ioh ia ka jingkoit jingkhiah, kaba long kaba donkam shibun 
eh. Kumta, ki la plie ia ki hospital bad dispensary ban

jingiasyllok society of Christ Jesus. Ha ka snem 1979, la 
seng ia ka Rapsbun School of Nursing, ha Laitumkhrah.

ka snem 1987, ia ka jingpyniaid ia kane ka hospital la 
shimti noh da ki sister Holy Cross. Ka Nazareth hospital 
ka pynbeit ruh ban ai jingsumar bad jinghikai sha ki 
nongkyndong da ki kali, kum sha Dawki, Nongstoin, Smit 
bad kiwei kiwei de ki bynta.

ha Jaiaw da ki sister ka jingiasyllok jong ka Mary Help of 
Christictns (M.S.M.H.C). Ka jingthmu jong kane ka long 
ban iarap ia kiba duk ba shitom bad ban ai jinghikai ia 
ki halor ka jingkoit jingkhiah.

Ha ki por mynshuwa, ki Khasi kim don jingthoh 
jingtar ne dak thoh la ka jong. Kumta haduh ka shiteng 
jong ka spah snem kaba la leit, ki ju pyndonkam da ki dak 
thoh jong ki para marjan. Dei haduh ka snem 1841, ba 
ngi la pyndonkam da ki dak thoh Roman. Ki mishonari 
jong ka Welsh Presbyterian Mission ki la wan ha ka snem 
1841, bad ki la wan rah shibun ki jingkylla ha ka thoh ka 
tar. Ki la sdang ban thoh da ki dak Roman ha ka rukom 
krcn Sohra ha ka jaka ki dak Bengalee. U Thomas Jones 
I u dei u nongprat lynti ha kane phang, bad la khot ia u, 
u kpa ki dak thoh Khasi. Kumta, ki Mishonari ryngkat bad 
ka jingpynphriang ia ka jingngeit Kristan, ki la ai ruh ia 
ka jingstad, ne ka thoh ka pule bad ka thoh ka tar. 

Ynda la mih noh ki phadar Salvatorian ha ka thma 
bah ba nyngkong, ia ka mission Katholik ia buh hapoh ka 
jingpeit jong ki phadar Jesuit. Ha ka snem 1922, la wan

sa ki mishonari jong ka Society of St. John Bosco kiba la 
tip kum ki Salesians. Kine ki mishonari ki la noh synniang 
radbah ha ka Hang ka thoh ka tar. Ki la pynmih bun tylli

kiba la pynroi shibun eh ia ka thoh ka tar Khasi. Ha ka 
snem 1922, ki la pynmih ia ka L^nti sha bneng bad ki 
Jingduwai, ka jingrwai Paidbab, ka kot Jingduwai na ka 
bynta ki khynnah, bad ka kot jingduwai bad Jingrwai ha 
u snem 1925 bad ka Jingtohkit ia ka jingshisha ha u snem 
1927.

pynmih ia ka kot ba la ai kyrteng, ki Dienjat ki Longshuwa.

4tarik u Kyllalyngkot 1908. U la pule ia ka Primary bad

iehnoh ia ka kam bad u la pdiang ia ka jingkhot jong u

dangrit, u Fr. Elias, u la long u ba trei shitom uba sngewrit,

KI KAMRAM BAN lADA lA LADE 

Ka jingkynshew kyndiap kan 
Pynlait langi Na Bun ki Jingeh. 

Man la u snem, la rakhe kyrpang ia ka National 
saving day nc ka sngi kynshew kyndiap Jong ka Ri ban 
pynkynmaw ia ka jingdonkam jong ka jingkynshew kyndiap, 
haka jingim jongngi. 

Ha kaba iadei bad kane, ka sorkar jylla, ka la pynbeit 
ia ka jingialang ryngkat bad ka national saving Organisation, 
kaban long haka 30 tarik u Risaw halor ka World Thrift 
day ne ka sngi ban kunai, jong ka pyrthei baroh kawei 
ban pynphriang ia ka khubor kumno ba ki paidbah, kin 
long kiba kunai, bad ban nang ban kynshew kyndiap. 

Ka kynshew kyndiap kam long kaba thymmai ha n^.

Naduh ki ix)r mynshuwa, ki kmie ki kpa tymmen, ki ju buh 
ne kynshew katto katne na ka jingioh na ka bynta ki por 
ki ban wan. Ki ju hikai ia ngi, ba ngin long kiba trei 
shitom, kiba ioh ia kiei kiei da ka jingtrei shitom, bad Idba 
kunai ha ka jingim jong ngi. Ngi dei ban kyndiap, ioh <kxi 
kum kano kano ka jingjia, kum ka pang ka shitom, ka lap 
ka im, ka jingshong kurim bad ka jingpule jong ki khun, 
ka iap thngan, bad kino kino ki jingjia jong ka mariang. 

Ha ki por mynshuwa, haba ym pat don pisa kum 
mynta, ki briew ki ju ia bujli mar kylliang da ki tiar Id tar 
bad kum ka jingdonkam jong ki ia kane ki khot ha ka ktien 
phareng ka "barter system". Hynrei, mynta ngi la pyndonkam 
da ka pisa, ban thied ia i jingdonkam jong ngi. Ha Id por 
mynshuwa, ki briew ki kynshew da kaba buh da ka k^r. 



ka nipa, ka khyndew, bad kiwei kiwei de. Hynrei naduh 
ba mih ka pisa, ka la kham pynsuk iaki paidbah. Da ka 
jingmih jong ki bank, bad kiwei kiwei pat ki jaka buh pisa, 
Id paidbah ki la suk ban buh bad kynshew pisa, ha kine 
Id jaka buh pisa, Lyngba ka pisa ngi lah ban thied bad ioh 
iaid ^gdonkam babun jong ngi. Ngi lah ban ioh shibun ki 
pngmyntoi, lada ngi buh bad kynshew pisa, ha ki bank bad 
kiwei kiwei pat ki jaka buh pisa. Ka jingkynshew pisa, ka 
iarap, ban pynroi ia ka pisa ba ng: buh, lyngba ka sut ba 
ngi ioh, ka long kumba ka nang pynheh shuh shuh ia ka 
kamai kajih jong ngi Ngi lait ruh na ka jingshah tuh, 
khamtam lei ha kine ki por mynta, ba la jyllei ka tuh ka 
thiem. Ka iada mh iangi na kmo kino ki jingjia kiba sniew, 
kum ka jingpluh ding. Hynrei haduh mynta, khamtam lei 
lei ha ki nongkyndong, ki briew ki dang kynshew da kaba 
buh ia ka pisa ha ki synduk, ne buh da ka ksiar ka rupa 
ne thied da ki jain ki nep. Kane ka wan rah ruh ia ka

jmg^er, bad ka jingduhnong, bad ka don ruh ka jingma, 
ioh don ka jingshah tuh, ne bam khniang ka pisa ha synduk 
ne kiwei kiwei de ki jingjia bad ngi lah ban duh noh, ia 
kaei kaei ba ngi la kamai shitom, ha ka shikhyllip mat. 

Ka jingkynshew pisa, ka long kaba donkam haduh 
katta katta, namar lada ngi buh ka pisa ha ing, kan lut 
phut bad kan nym neh, namar kat shaba ngi phai, ki bun 
jingdonkam suda, bad ngi shu ikwah lut ia kine baroh, bad 
Id juigdawa Id long ki ba bun. Hynrei, lada ngim don pisa 
ha ing, ka shu biang la ka biang hi, namar ngi la nang ban 
pyniaid kat kum ka jingdon jong ngi. Ka jingkynshew pisa 
jong hciki bank ka pynroi ruh ia ka ioh ka kot jong ka 
jylla, bad jong ka Ri hi baroh kawei. Ki bank bad kiwei 
kiwei Id jaka buh pisa ki lah ban airam kylliemg da ka pisa 
ba ngi buh, ia ki briew ki ban seng ia ki kaun lajong, kum 
Id karkheuia rit bad kiba heh, ban thied ing, thied kali, bad 



kiwei kiwei de ki jingdonkam. Kumta ka Ri jong ngi ka 
lah ban tur shaphrang ha ki kam pynroi bad ka ioiv ka kot. 
Kumta, kiba buh pisa bad kiba shim ram na ki bank ki 
ia ioh jingmyntoi iang, bad ka wanrah ruh ia ka jingiohnong 
na ka pisa ba ngi buh bzm ioh jingmyntoi lang baroh. Ki 
don shibun ki lad kynshew pisa, kum ki bank bad kiwei 
de ki jaka buh pisa kum ki Post Office, ki Insurance bad 
kumta tur ter, la ka jingtip ba kham bniah, ngi lah ban 
ioh na ki ophis jong ki. Ki National savings, ki la pynmih 
bun ki lad ban kynshew pisa, bad, ioh jingmyntoi kham 
bun, kum ka Indira Vikas patra, Kisan Vikas Patra, National 
sawng Certificate bad kiwei kiwei de ki lad kiba ngim lah 
ban niew lut hangne. Ki bank ruh ki pynmih bun ki rukom 
buh pisa kum ki Current Account kiba ngi lah ban shim 
pisa ha kano kano ka por, ki Fixed Deposit, kat kum ki

por ba ngi kwah ban buh bad ka sut ruh ka long ka ba 
kham heh, ka Recurring Deposit kaba ngi lah ban buh man 
u bnai, bad kiwei kiwei de. Ki briew ki lah ban siew ruh 
da ka cheque. Bank Draft ha ka jaka ka pisa, khnang ban 
lait ban rah pisa bad lait na ki jingma. Haba leit jingleit, 
lah ruh ban rah da ki Traveller's Cheque ha ka jaka ka pisa. 

Ka kynshew ka kyndiap, ka long ka ba donkam 
haduh katta katta, namar kata, ngi dei ban pynlong ka 
jingmlien, ban kynshew wat la i long i barit katno katno 
ruh, hynrei yn sa nang roi shuh shuh. Peit ia ka nuksa jong 
u ngap, u ba her na uwei u syntiew sha uwei pat, beui kjit 
ia ka um bathiang ban pynlong ia ka ngap, khnang ba un 
ioh ban bam ha ka por bym ioh um syntiew. Kane ka dei 
ka nuksa ia ngi ruh kumjuh. 

Ki kynthei, ki don ka bynta kaba khraw tarn, ha kaba 
kyntiew ia ka ioh ka kot, bad ka imlemg ka sahlang. Ki 
dei ban nang ban kynshew ban kyndiap, ban kunai khnang 
ban nym khuslai. Ki dei ban hikai ruh, ia la ki khun ki kti. 



Ki kynthei ki nang ban bynta iaka pisa, kumno ban pynlut 
kat kum ka jingdonkam, ha ing ha sem. Ki nang ban kunai 
ha ka ba iadei bad ka siew ka tda, ka shet ka tiew khlem 
pynsep ei. Ka jingpynlut jong ngi, ka del ban long kat kum 
u pud jong ka jingioh kamai Jong ngi, ngim dei ban kwah 
rhah palat ia kiei kiei. Ngim dei ban bam hati kit kulai 
kumba ong ki Khasi, ne ban pynlut palat ia kaba ngi ioh. 
Mynta ki briew ki la kham sngewthuh ia ka jingdonkam 
ban kynshew kyndiap, hynrei dang donkam ban pynphriang 
shuh shuh ia kane ka jingdonkam. Ki Bank, Ki Post Office 
bad kiwei kiwei de ki jaka buh pisa ki dei ban ai bun ki 
jingpynshai, halor kaba iadei bad ka kynshew kyndiap, bad

ki lad ai jingiarap ki bapher, khnang ba ki paidbah mh kin 
tip. Khatduh eh, ngi kyrpad ia ki paidbah, ba kin pynlong 
ka jingmlien, ban kynshew kyndiap khnang ba ngin lah ban 
lait na bun ki jingeh ha ka jingim jong ngi. 



Ki hok ki nongthied nongpet 


Nyngkong eh, ngi dei, ban tip bad sngewthuh, ba ki 
nongthied nongpet, nongsiew nongtda ia kino kino ki tiar, 
la ki dei ki marbam mardih, ne ki tiar ba ngi pyndonkam, 
ne kaba wai ia kano kano ka kam, ne jii^ei bad ka 
jingmyntoi ba ioh na ki, dei mangi ki paidbah kiba siew 
pisa na ka bynta ki jingdonkam Jong n^. Ngi don hok ruh 
ban ioh, ia ki mar ki mata kiba iadei dor, bad ka pisa ba 
ngi siew, bad ngi don ruh ki hok ban iada ia Iade.l4 thied 
ka pet ka siew ka tda ka ktah ia ka ioh ka kot Jong baroh, 
naduh u baduk basuk, haduh uba riewspah, naduh u 
nongbylla sngi, haduh u nong trci Sorkar ne uba trei kam 
lajong. Wat kito kiba pynmih hi ia ki tiar ki tar ruh, ki 
hap ban thied jingthied, namar kim lah satia ban pynmih 
lut, ia ki tiar baroh kat kaba ki donkam. 

Man la ka por, ngi iohsngew, ba ki nongthied 
nongpet, nongsiew nongtda, ki shah khniot bein shah thok, 
da kaba ai ki tiar ki bym ia dei satia bad ka dor ba la 
siew, ne da ki mar ki bym long. Nangta sa ka 
jingpynkiewdor, ka jingduna thew bad kiwei kiwei de. Wat 
la ngi la siew ruh, ngim ioh satia ka jinghun, kat kum ka 
jingsiew. 

Ha ka jingshisha, haba ngi rung ha ki dukan ngim 
dei ban sngew ba, ngi hap pyndem ha ka mon u nongdie,

hynrei u nongthied u dei ban long kynrad halor ka iew ka 
hat. U Mahatma Gandhi u ong ba, "U Nongthied u dei u 
nongwankai uba donkam tarn ha ki khyrdop Jong ngi, ym 
dei ba u shaniah ha ngi, hynrei dei mcin^ kiba shaniah ha 
u. Ymdei ba u pynwit ia ki kam Jong ngi, dei na ka bynta 
Jong u, ngi trei. Ym dei ba ngi iarap ia u, da kaba ngi 
pyni, ne shakri ia u. Dei ma u uba ai jingmyntoi ia ttgi 
da kaba u shah ia ngi ban leh kumta". 



Don katto katne ki hok png ki nongthied nongpet, 
nongsiew nongtda bad nong pyndonkam: 

1. Ka hole ban iada > kaba mut ban iada ia tade da kaba 
ieng pyrshah ia ki jingdie ia ki tiar, bad jingpyntrei ia ki 
kam kiba pynmynsaw iangi. 

2. Ka hok ban tip - kaba mut, ba ngi dei ban tip ia ka 
jinglong jingbha, jingneh slem, ka thew ka woh, ka dor jong 
ki mar Id mata khnang ban lait na ki jingieh bym hok ki 
nongkhaii pateng. 

3. Ka hok ban jied - ngi don ka hok ban ioh jied ia ki 
tiar ki tar bad ki mar ki mata ha ka dor bapher bapher. 

4. Ka hok ban pynpaw ne ban pyni bad kdew - Kane 
ka mut, ba ki nongthied ki lah ban pynsngew, ia ki jingud 
jong Id, ha kino kino ki bor ba dei peit. 

5. Ka hok ban wad jingiarap - kaba mut, ban wad 
jingiarap bad ban pynbeit ban pynbha, ia keino kano ka 
jingieh bymhok bad bymdei ha ka khaii pateng, lane ka 
jingshah ban bein ki nongthied nongpet.

6. Ka hok ban ioh pnghikai • Kane ka dei ka hok, ban 
ioh ia ka jinghikai bad jingtip kaba pura bad paka, ha ka 
liang ka thied ka pet. Ngi shem ba kawei, na ki daw ba 
kongsan eh jong ka jingshah lehbein ki nongthied ki nongpet, 
ka long ka jingbieit khamtam ha nongkyndong, ha kaba 
dang bun ki bym pat nangthoh nangpuie. 

Kum ki nongthied ki nongpet, ngi dei ban tip ruh ia 
ki kamram, bad ban sngewthuh ia ki katto katne ki rukom 
thied jingthied. 

1. Ngi dei ban tip, ia ki dor ki mur jong ki tiar ki tar, ki 
mar ki mata ba die ha ki dukan, bad ban husiar bad peit 
bniah ruh ia ki dor, ba shutah da ki nongdie pateng. 

2. Ban peit bniah ruh khamtam ia ki marbam mardih, ba 
ioh don ki jingkhleh lang bad ki jingbam kiba sniew kum 
ha ka dud, musli musla, umphniang bam, khaw, shini bad 
kiwei kiWei de. 



3. Ngi del ban pcit bha, ban pyndonkam ia ki marbam 
mardih kat kum ka por ba la buh (manufacturing and 
expiring date). Kine ki long khamtam kum ki dawai, 
jingbam khyllung, doh ne dohkha tin, jingbam tin jingbam 
jingdih ha ki synduk kot ne ha ki bilor. 

4. Ngi dei ban long Idba husiar, bad peit bniah ia ki tiar 
ki tar kiba shu pynmih thok, kiba ai kyrteng bad pyrthuh 
ia ki company ba pawnam, tang ban lah die bad iohnong. 

5. Ngi dei ban pan "Cash Memo" ne rusit ia ki tiar ba ngi 
thied, khnang ba ngin don sabut, ban iaieh pyrshah, ia 
kano kano ka jtngleh bymhok. Ngi dei ban don ruh ka por

ban thied jingthied, ym ban shu thied kyrkieh. 

Khnang ban iada ia ki nongthied nongpet, nongsiew 
nongtda, nongbam nongdih, bad nong pyndonkam, ka Sorkar 
India, ka la pynmih ia ka Consumer's Protection Act, 
1936, bad la nang kham pynbha ia kane ka ain ha u snem 
1993, da kaba kham pynheh, bad kham pyntrei kam shuh 
shuh. Kane ka ain ka long, kaba donkam haduh katta 
katta, ban iada bad iarap ia ki nongthied nongpet, na kino 
kino ki jingleh bymhok ne ban-bein bad khwan myntoi sha 
ba palat, jong ki nongkhaii ha ka die ka thied, kane ka 
jingiada ka kynthup ia ki jaid jingthied baroh bad jaid 
jingtrei ha kaba ki nongthied ne nongpyndonkam ki siew 
pisa. Kat kum kane ka ain, ka don ka Central Consumer 
Protection Council bad ki State Consumer Protection Council, - 
ban iarap bad iada ia ki hok ki nongthied nongpet. Ki don 
ruh ki Ingshari ban bishar halor ki jingujor jong ki nongthied 
nongpet nongsiew nongtda. Ki don ki Consumer Dispute 
Redressal Commission kiba ngi tip kum ka National 
Commission bad State Commission bad ki Consumer 
Disputes Redressal Forum, ne District Forum. Kine ki 
Ingshari ki ai jingiarap bad jingiada, ia kino kino .ki jingujor 
jong ki nongthied nongpet, bad, iada na ki jingleh bymhok 
jong ki katto katne ki nongkhaii na ka bynta ban khwan 
jingmyntoi iaiade. 



Hynrei, ngi shem ba dang bun ki nongthied nongpet, 
nongsiew nongtda, kiba shah ban bein bad shedi shim kabu, 
wat la don ka ain jingiada kum ka Consumers Protection 
Act, la bun snem mynta. Balei ka long kumne? ne kaei ka 
daw ? La ka long lehse ba ka ^gbun ki rukom bad kyllain 
bft hap iaid lyngba da ki ingshari, ne ba ki paidbah kim 
kwah, ban khih, kim dcm jingsngew, ne kim kwah, bad kim

shlur ne kut jingmut ban iaieh pyrshah ia ki jingshah ban 
bein ne khnot bein. Ka lah ban long ruh ba ngi don ka 
jingmiien bad ap ba kin leh Idwei pat. Ka jingiada ba ka 
Sorkar ka ai, lU thmu ia kaba bha, bad ka long kaba 
kordor haduh katta katta. Hynrei, ka Ri India ba iar Jong
