Ka bynta jong ngi ban pynkhuid ia lade, ia ki iing 
ki sem bad ka shnong ka thaw - 

Ka don ka jingong ba ka jingkhuid ne jingleh khuid 
ka iajan bha bad U Blei (cleanliness is next to Godliness) 
bad 1^ jabieng kaba stad ka shong ha ka met kaba khuid 
(a sound mind resides in a sound body). Kumta ruh ngi dei 
ban long kiba khuid, namar kiei kiei baroh ki sdang na la 
ing. Ngi dei ban leh khuid ia lade, ban bam ki jingbam kiba 
khuid, ban phong ki Jain kiba khuid. Pynkhuid bha iala ka 
rum iing neng iing, thung ki jhur ki jhep,ki syntiew, ki 
dieng bad ki diengsoh haba don jaka. Sar khuid ia ki nala 
bad jaka leit shabar. Ym dei ban pyntuid ia ki jakhlia sha

compound jong kiwei pat ki briew ne bein sar niut bad theh 
niut sha surok tang bcui lait na lade. Don ki briew kiba leh 
kumta bad lada ong ruh ki lehdom pynbeurt. Ngi dei ban 
don ka jingsngewthuh briew ban nym bret niut sha lynti 
syngkien bad ban hikai ruh ia ki khun naduh dangrit ban 
lehkhuid. Ngi don ka bynta kaba khraw tarn ban pynneh 
pynsah ia ka jingitynnad ka mariang da kaba sdang na 
lade, lym kumta ki longdien mandien jong ngi kin sa tim 
ia ngi. Ngi dei ban peit ba ka jingpynheh iing jong ngi kan 
nym ktah iaka koit ka khiah, dei ban don ki jaka leit 
shabar bad nala kiba bieung. Don napdeng jong ngi lehse 
ngi shu shna khlem pyrkhat ia ki iing bad ki dukan tang 
ban ioh pisa. Ngi dei ban pynbeit ia ki rukom jingiatrei 
lang ne trei paidbah. Kum ha kaba pynkhuid dong, ne 
shnong, ki lynti ki syngkien bad kiwei kiwei de. Ki komiti 
dong, dorbar shnong ki dei ban pynmih ki ain shnong kiba 
biang ban leh ia kane.

ki Hima Khasi) ha kawei na ki jingkren ha Shillong ba ka 
Ri long ngi ka dei ka Ri ba shong ki Blei, ka Iyer ba 
pyngngad, ki lum ki wah ba ithiang, ka um kaba shngiam 
ka ban khring wat ia ki Blei ruhi ban shong hangne lada 
ki dohnud jong ngi ki khuid. 

Ka Sorkar ka dei ban wad ki *lad ki lynti kat kum ki 
^ngsteKl ba mynta ban pynneh pynsah ia ka jingkhuid bad

tip ia kane.

VIII 

KA INDIA BAD KA ROI KA PAR 

KA JINGIATYLLI JONG KA RI INDIA, BAD 
KA RITI SYNSHAR NE CONSTITUTION 

Kum kiwei pat ki nongsepngi, ki Phareng, ki la wan 
^ Ri India jong-ngi kum ki nongkhaii, hynrei ki la kylla 
long ki nongsynshar, bad ki la ^shar ia ngi kumba arspah 
snem eiei. Hynrei da ka jingialeh shitom, bad len lade, jong 
kiba bun ki nongialam jong ngi, ryngkat bad ki paidbah, 
ha ka por jong ka jingialeh ban ioh ia ka jinglaitluid, ba 
ngi la ioh kheit mynta ia u soh jong ka jinglaitluid. Ka 
Sorkar phareng, da ka ain kaba la pynrung da ka Parliament 
jong ka Ri Bilat, ka la ai ka jinglaitluid, ia ka Ri India jong 
ngi ha ka 15 tarik u Nailar, 1947. Kumta, ka Ri India, 
ka la kylla long ka Ri synshar paidbah, kaba heh tarn ha 
ka pyrthei, ha ka 26 tarik u Kyllayngkot, 1950, ha kaba, 
ka Ri baroh kawei ka la pdiang ia ka Constitution ne ka 
Riti synshar. 

Ka Constituent Assembly, ka la thaw bad pynwandur 
ia ka Riti synshar jong ka Ri India. La don ki Committee 
bapher bapher jong ka Constituent Assembly, kum ka

Union Constitution Committee, ka Union Power Committee, 
Cbmmittee on Fundamental Rights. Hadien ka jingiatainia, 
halor ki Report jong kine ki Committee bapher, ka 
Constituent Assembly, ka sa thung ia ka Drafting 
Committee, ha ka 29 tarik U Nailar 1947. U Chairman 
jong ka Drafting Committee u dei u Dr. B.R. Ambedkar. 
la ka Draft Constitution jong ka Ri India, la pynmih ha U 
Naiwieng, 1948, ban peit bniah bad ia tainia bin pa bin 
halor jong ka. Hadien bun ki jingiashong, bad jingiatainia. 



ka Constituent Assembly, ka la shong khatduh eh ha ka 
14 tarik U Naiwieng, na ka bynta ka Third Reading, bad 
ka la kut ha ka 26 tarik U Naiwieng, 1949. Dei ha kane 
ka sngi ba la ioh ia ka jingsoi U President ka AssemUy, 
bad la pdiang ia ka. 

Ka Riti synshar jong ka Ri India, ka la mih, na ka 
jingwad bniah, bad ki jingiatainia ba janai jong ki 
nongmihkhmat ba shemphang jong ki paidbah, kiba kwah 
ban pynbit pynbiang, ia ka rukom synshar khadar. Kumta, 
ka Riti synshar jong ka Ri India jong ngi, ka dei ka jong 
u paidbah, da u paidbah, bad na ka bynta u paidbah, 
lyngba ki nongmihkhmat ba stad jong ki, ha ka Constituent 
Assembly, bad ka long ka Riti synshar kaba jrong shibun 
eh. Ki Kyntien sdang jong ka Riti synshar ki ong "We the 
people of India", kaba mut, ma ngi ki briew jong ka«Ri 
India, kaba pyni, ba ka dei ka Riti synshar jong ki p^jdbzdi. 

Na ka 'Preamble' ne ka lamphrang jong ka Riti synsher, 
ngi lah ban iohi ia ka hok ka jinglaitluid, ha ka imlang 
sahlang, ka ioh ka kot, bad ka sain hima sima. Ka jinglaitluid 
ha ka pyrkhat pyrdain, ka kren ka khana, ka jingngeit bad 
jingmane ia kano kano ka niam ka rukom, ka jingiaryngkat

ha ka kyrdan, bad ki lad jong uwei pa uwei u nongshong 
shnong. ka Riti synshar ka kweih ban ai, bad pynioh ia ka 
jingsngew bad jingialong shipara. Da ka Constitution Forty 
Second Amendment Act, 1976, la buh shuh sa kine ki 
kyntien ha ka lamphrang, "Unity and Integrity of the nation" 
kaba mut, ka jingialong kawei bad ka jingiatylli jong ka Ri. 
Ha ka part - III jong ka Fundamental Rights, ka Article 14 
jong ka Constitution, ka ai ia ka jingia ryngkat kyrdan, ha 
man la u nongshong shnong, ha khmat ka ain, ne ka 
iingiada hapoh ka ain ha Ri India, kaba ki ong ha ka ktien 
phareng "Equality before Law". Ka Article 15 ka ong ba, 
vn nym don satia ka jingiapher, ha ki niam, ki jaidbimriew, 


ka jinglong kynthei ne shynrang, ka ai ia ka kyrdan ba 
ia ryngkat, ha ka liang ka kam, khlem ka jingpyniapher, 
ha ka liang ka niam, jinglong jaidbynriew, kynthei, shynrang 
bdd ka jaka bakha, ka ain ka dei ban long ia baroh. Ka 
Article 16, ka ai ia ka kyrdan ba ia ryngkat, ha ka liang 
ka kam, khlem ka jingpyniapher, ha ka liang ka niam, 
jinglong jaidbynriew, kynthei, shynrang bad ka jaka bakha. 
Ka Article 17, ka kwah ban pynduh noh ia ka jingbymbit 
ktah ne "Untouchability" bad yn pynshitom ruh kat kum ka 
Ain na ka bynta kane. 

Kumta, kine ki Article 14,15,16 bad 17 jong ka 
Fundamental Rights, ki long kiba kordor ha, kaba, ki 
nongthaw ia ka Riti synshar, ki la ai ka kyrdan ba ia 
ryngkat, ha khmat ka ain bad ka jingiada da ka ain. Ki 
ai ruh ia ka jingiaryngkat kyrdan, ha ka imiang sahlang, ka 
ioh ka kot, ka kam ka jam, ha baroh ki nongshong shnong 
jong ka Ri India, khlem don ka jingiapher hapdeng ka 
jinglong jaidbynriew, ka niam ka rukom jinglong kynthei ne 
shynrang bad ka jaka ba kha.

la ka jingialong kawei bad jingsngew shipara, la ialeh 
ban wan rah, da kaba pynlong ia ka Ri India ka "Secular 
State", kaba mut ba ka Ri India, ka ai lad bad iada ia 
baroh ki jaidniam, bad ym don kawei ruh ka jaidniam, 
kaba ngi lah ban ong ba, ka dei ia ka Ri baroh kawei. la 
kane ka jinglaitkiid, ha ka rukom mane Blei, la buh ha ki 
Article 25-28, kiba ailad ia uwei pa uwei u nongshong 
shnong ban bud ia kano kano ka rukom mane Blei ne 
rukom jingngeit Blei. 

Ha ka Part - IV jong ka "Directive Principles" of State 
Policy, ki don ki kyndon ne bynta, kiba bthah ia ka State, 
nalor kiwei kiwei pat, ban shim katto katne ki lad, ha kaba 
iadei bad ki kynthei, ka imiang sahlang bad ka ioh ka kot. 
Ka Article 39 (a), ka ong, ba baroh ki nongshong shnong 



kynthei bad shynrang kumjuh, ki don ka hok ban trei ban 
ktah ban im. Ka Article 39 (d) ka ong ba, dei ban don ka 
juh ka jingsiew, na ka bynta ka juh ka kam, ia baroh 
kynthei bad shynrang. Ka Article 29 (1), ka ai ban pynneh 
pynsah ia kino kino ki rit paid (minorities) ha kano kano 
ka bynta, jong ka Ri India kiba don la ka jong ka ktien 
ka thylliej ka thoh ka tar bad ka Riti ka dustur. 

la ka Part - IV - A jong ka Fundamental Duties 
Article 51-A, la pynrung da ka Constitution forty Second 
Amendment Act, 1976. Kat kum kane ka bynta, ka dei, 
ka kamram jong uwei pa uwei u nongshong shnong, jong 
ka Ri India, nalor kiwei pat, ban im bad bud kat kum ka 
Riti synshar, ban burom ia ka Lama jong ka Rk bad ka 
National Anthem. Dei ban iada ia ka jingiatylli jong ki 
briew, ha ka Ri India baroh kawei. Dei ban khein kor ia 
ki Riti ki dustur, bad kiei kiei kiba ngi la iohpateng na ki

long shuwa manshuwa, bad ki Kolshor bapher bapher jong 
ka Ri. Ki "Directive Principles of State Policy", Ki long ki 
tynrai ba donkam ha ka synshar khadar jong ka Ri. 

Ka jingiatylli jong ka Ri, ka long kaba donkam haduh 
katta katta. Kumta ki nongthaw ia ka Riti synshar, ki la 
buh ia ka jingsngew ia long shipara ne (Fraternity). Kane 
ka jingsngew, ngin ioh lada weng noh ia ka jingbym bit 
ktah (untouchability) jingkhim mynsiem, jingsngew pher ka 
jaid ka kynja, ka jingsngew pher ki jaka ne jylla, khamteun 
ki jingsngew pyrshah ia la ka Ri, kiba pynwit bad pynthut 
ia ka jinglong kawei jong ka Ri India. 

Ka lamphrang jong ka Riti synshar ka long kaba 
kordor haduh katta katta. Dei lyngba jong ka, ba ngi lah 
ban thew bad iohi ia ka dor jong ka Riti synshar, ka kwah 
ban pynurlong ia ki kyntien jong u Mahatma Gandhi, u Kpa 
jong ka jaidbynriew, ha kaba u kren ha ka jingthoh, "India 
of my dreams", u kwah ba kiba duk tarn kin sngew ba. 



ki don ka sur ban kren, ka India ha kaba baroh. ki 
jaidbynriew kin im ha ka suk ka sain, ym dei ban don jaka 
ha India ia ka jingbymbit ktah bad ka jingdih buaid. bad 
ba, ka India kan long ka jaka, ha kaba ki kynthei kin don 
ki juh ki hok, kum ki shynrang. Ka jingsynshar paidbah 
kam lah ban suhthied bad skhem, lada, kam lah ban 
wanrah ia ka jingsngew shipara, ba ngi ia long ki khun ki 
ksiew jong kawei ka Ri India. 

Ka jingsngew bad jingialong shipara ka long, kaba 
donkam haduh katta katta, namar ka Ri India jongngi, ka 
long kaba heh, bad kaba iar shibun eh, ka don shibun ki 
jaidbynriew ki bapher bapher, kiba don la ki jong ki Riti

ki Dustur, ki niam ki rukom, ki ktien ki thylliej, bad la ki 
jong ki riam ki beit. Hynrei, wat hapdeng ki jingiapher kiba 
bun katne, ka don ka jingiatylli, kaba ha ka ktien Phreng, 
ki ong "Unity in Diversity". Ki jaidbynriew bapher jong ka 
Ri India, ki don ha la ki thain ki thain, bad ki jylla, kiba 
long, kum ki syntiew ba bun rong, ha la ki jong ki jong 
ki jingitynnad, ki pynphuh pynphieng bad pynitynnad ia ka 
Ri India bakhraw jong ngi. Ngi dei ban ieit bad ban don 
ka jingsngew trai ia la ka Ri. Ngin burom ia la ka jong ka 
Riti ka dustur, ka niam ka rukom, ka ktien ka thylliej, ka 
riam ka beit, ka put ka tern, ka shad ka kmen, bad ban 
pynneh ia ki. Hynrei ka jingieit ia la ka jong, kam dei ban 
pynlong ia ngi kiba khim mynsicm, bad ban isih ia ka jong 
kiwei pat, namar ka Riti synshar ka ai ka jinglaitluid ia 
baroh ki nong India. Ka dei ka kamram jong ngi, ba ngin 
ialeh barabor ban wan rah ia ka jingiatylli jong ka Ri India 
baieit jong ngi. 


Ka Jingmut Jong Ka Republic Day 


M 2 UI la u snem, ka Ri India jong ngi, ka ju rakhe 
pyndonburom ia ka 26 tarik u Kyllalyngkot, kutn ka sngi 
Republic Day. Kane ka sngi ka long kaba khraw haduh 
katta katta, bad ka ban sah jingkynmaw ha ka history jong 
ka Ri India bad jong ka pyrthei hi baroh kawei. Dei ha 
kane ka sngi, ha ka snem 1950 ba la sdang ne pyntreikam 
ia ka Constitution ne ka Riti Synshar jong ka Ri India, bad 
ka Ri India ka la kylla long ka Ri Synshar paidbah kaba 
heh tarn ha ka pyrthei. 

Ki Phareng kiba la wan kum ki nongkhaii, hadien Id 
la kylla long ki nongsynshar ia ka Ri India jong ngi bun bun 
snem. Ki paidbah jong ka Ri India ki ,1a ia khih ban ioh

ia ka jinglaftluid. Ka Sorkar Phareng, ka la ai jinglaitluid 
ia ka Ri India ha ka 15 tarik u Nailar, 1947. Ha kane ka 
sngi ka Ri India baroh kawei ka .dap da ka jingkmen kaba 
khraw ban ioh ia ka jinglaitluid. Ka jingshah shitom bad 
shah lehbein bad ban bein jong ki nongialam bad ki paidbah 
ka la kut noh, bad ka jingphohsniew bad jingangnud jong 
ki ban ioh ia ka jinglaitluid bad ka jingsynshar da lade hi 
ka la urlong. 

Ha shuwa ban ioh ka jinglaitluid, ha ka snem 1947, 
ka sorkar phareng ka la phah ia ka Cabinet Mission sha 
India ha u snem 1946, ban ia kren bad ki nonglalarn na 
ka bynta ban ai noh ia ka bor synshar sha^ ki nong India.

Constituent Assembly ne ka Dorbar bah. Ka Constituent 
Assembly, ka la ia kynduh ha ka 9 tarik u Nohprah, 1946, 
bad ha ka 11 tarik la jied bad mynjur ba u Dr. Rajendra 
Prasad, un long kum u permanent Chairman jong ka 
Constituent Assembly. Hadien kata, la thung ia ka 
Committee na ka bynta ki rules, procedures, kaba kynthup 
15 ngut ki dkhot. La pdiang ia ki rules ba la wan rah da 
ka Committee ha ka 23 tarik u Nohprah, 1946. la u 
Chairman, la khot noh da u President naduh kata hi ka 
por ba la mynjur ia ki rules. 

Hadien ba la rai ia ka Indian Independence Act, ha 
u Naitung 1947, da ka Parliament jong ka Ri Bilat la buh 
ia ka 15 tarik u Nailar 1947, kum ka sngi ban ai noh ia 
ka jingsynshar sha ki Nong India. Kumta, ka Constituent 
Assembly, ka la thung ia kawei ka Committee, kaba

kynthup ia ki katto katne ngut ki riewpawkhmat. Kawei 
na ki kam ba kongsan tarn, jong kane ka Committee, ka 
long ban khmih shaphang ki kam jong ka Constituent 
Assembly kat kum ba la thoh ha ka Indian Indepiendence 
Act, 1947. la ka report la wan rah ha khmat ka Constituent 
Assembly ha ka 29 tarik u Nailar 1947 bad hadien ka 
jingiatainnia, ka Constituent Assembly ka la pdiang ia ka 
resolution ne ka rai kaba ia dei bad kane ka kam. Kawei 
na ki rai jong ka Constituent Assembly, ka long ban bteng 
bad pyndep ia ka kam kum ka Dominion Legislature haduh 
ban da lah ban pynlong ia ka parliament. la u nongpyniaid 
ia ka Constituent Assembly, kaba shong kum ka Constitution 
Making body, la khot u President bad ia u nongpyniaid jong 
ka Constituent Assembly haba ka shong kum ka Dominion 
Legislature, la khot u Speaker. U Dr. Rajendra Prasad u 
dei u president jong ka Constitution Meeting Body bad u 
Mr. G.V. Mavalankar u dei u Speaker jong ka Constituent 
Assembly kum ka Dominion Legislature. 





Katba ka Constituent Assembly, kam pat lah ban 
I haw ia ka Constitution ne Riti Synshar jong ka Ri India,

Government of India Act, 1935 bad ka India (Central

Governor General kaba la khot ka India Provisional

of India Notification. 

la ka Constitution jong ka India la shna ne pynwandur 
da ki briew jong ka Ri India lyngba ki nongmihkhmat ba

bapher-bapher jong ka Constituent Assembly. Hadien ka

jong ka Drafting Committee u dei u Dr. B.R. Ambedkar.

1948. Ka Constituent Assembly, ka la shong ha u 'Naiwieng

Naiwieng na ka bynta ka "Third reading " ne ka jingpule 
kaba lai bad ka la kut ha ka 26 tarik u Naiwieng, 1947. 
Dei ha kane ka sngi ba la ioh ia ka jingsoi jong u President 
ka Constituent Assembly, bad la diang ia ka. Ka jingiakynduh 
ba khatduh eh. jong ka Constituent Assembly ka la long 
ha ka 24 tarik u Kyllalyngkot, 1950, na ka bynta ban ia 
soi ia ka Constitution. Don katto katne ki bynta ba la 
sdang ban pyntrei kam noh, naduh ka 26 tarik u Naiwieng, 

1949. Hynrei baroh kawei ka Constitution, ka la sdang trei 
kam naduh ka 26 tarik u Kvllalvnqkot 1950, kaba ngi tip 
kiim ka Republic Dav. la kane ka tarik la kdew ha ka

commencement" ne ka sngi jong ka jingsdang pyntrei kam

ba nyngkong eh jong ka Ri India la pynlong ha ka snem

1952. 

La thaw ne pynwandur ia ka Constitution jong ka Ri 
India da ki nongmihkhmat jong ki paidbah ha ka Constituent 
Assembly. Ka Constitution, ka la mih da kaba shim na ki 
Constitution jong kiwei pat ki Ri ryngkat bad ka jingwad 
bniah bad ki jingiatai nia ba janai jong kine ki 
nongmihkhmat, kiba kwah ban pynbit pynbiang ia ka 
rukom synshar khadar. Kumta, ka Constitution jong ka Ri 
India ka dei ka jong u paidbah, da u paidbah bad na ka 
bynta u paidbah bad ka long ka Constitution kaba jrong 
shibun eh. 

Ka Ri India, ka long ka nuksa jong ka jingsynshar 
paidbah kaba khraw tarn ha ka pyrthei. Ka Ri India ka la 
ioh ka ^glaitluid lyngba ka ^gshitom bad len lade jong ki 
million ki paidbah, kynthei bad shynrang ryngkat bad ki 
nongialam. Ka dei ka kamram jong iwei pa iwei na ngi 
ban pynneh ia kata ka jinglaitluid bad ka jingsynshar paidbah 
bad ban nang pynskhem ia ki. Ngin kynmaw ia ka 
jingkren jong u Pandit Jawaharlal Nehru, uba long u Myntri 
Rangbahduh ba nyngkong jong ka Ri India kiba u la kren 
ha^ ka 14 tarik Nailar, 1947, shuwa ban ioh ia ka jinglaitluid. 
U la ong, ba haba ka baje ka tied ha ka por shiteng synnia, 
katba ka pyrthei ka dang ioh thiah ka Ri India pat ka la 
kyndit thiah ha ka jingim kaba thymmai bad ka jinglaitluid. 
Ka por ka la poi ha kaba ym ju kham jia ha ka histoty 
ha kaba ngin jam na ka barim sha kaba thymmai, bad ka 
Ri kaba la shah ban bein ka la laitluid. U la ong ruh, ba 



ha kane ka por ngin kut jingmut ban aiti ia lade na ka 
bynta ka Ri bad ki briew jong ka.

Ki don mh shibun ki jingeh kiba ka Ri India jong ngi 
ka hap ban ia kynduh. Katto katne ki long, kum ka ^gduk 
ka jingkyrduh, ka jingbymiohkam ioh jam, ki jingkylla kiba 
wan ha ka ioh ka kot, ka imiang-sahlang bad ka synshar 
khadar, ki jingiapher jingmut, ka jingsngewpher ki jaid ki 
kynja, ki niam ki mkom, ki kam pynma bad pyntriem, ki 
jingpang jingshitom, ka jingbun jong ki bym nang thoh 
nangpule bad sa kiwci kiwei de. Kumta, kum ka jingrakhe, 
ia ka Republic Day ngin pyrshang bad kut jingmut ban 
nang pynkhlain shuh shuh ia ka Ri India baieid jong ngi ba 
kan nang leih ban iaid bad tur shaphrang kum ka Ri synshar 
paidbah ka ba khraw tarn. Ngin iai kynmaw ia ki kyntien 
kiba u Mahatma Gandhi u la kren kiba ong. " Mano ban 
im lada ka India ka iap bad mano ban iap lada ka India 
ka im." 



Ka Ri India ba laitluid kat kum ka 
jingiohi jong u Gandhi. 


La kha ia u Mohandas Karamchand Gandhi, ha ka 
2 tank u Risaw 1869, ha Gujerat. U la leit pule pyntbit 
ia ka jingstad ain sha Bilat. Ynda u la dep pule, u la wan 
phai sha Ri India. U la sdang ban trei kam kum u Riewstad 
ain ha Rajkot, nangta ha Bombay. Hadien khyndiat por, 
u la leit trei noh sha South Africa. Dei hangne, ba u la 
iohi ia ka jingshah lehbein ki nong India ha ki dohlieh. U 
Mahatma Gandhi u la sdang ban shakri ia ka jaitbynriew. 
U la sdang ruh ia ka Indian National Congress, bad u la 
shah kem bad shah set patok. U pyrshah ruh ia ka Asiatic 
Black Act, bad ia ka Transvaal Immigration Act. U la 
sdang ia ka jingialeh da ka lynti ba jai jai, bad khlem 
pynmynsaw iano iano ruh (nonviolence). U Gandhi u la

sdang pyntrei kam, ia ki rukom pyrkhat jong u, ban jop 
ia ka jingshah ban bein, bad leh thombor ka sorkar Phareng. 
Ka Sorkar South Africa, ka hap ban sngap ia ka sur jong 
u, bad weng noh shibun ki ain pyrshah ia ki nong India 
ha ka snem 1914. 

U Gandhi u la wan phai biang sha la ka jong ka Ri, 
bad u la sdang ban pynkylla ia ka rukom ialeh ban ioh ia 
ka jinglaitluid, jong ka Ri India na ki Phareng U la pynshong 
nongrim ia ka jingialeh halor ka jingshisha, ka hok, ka 
jingiaishah, khlem pyndonkam da ka bor, ka wait ne ka 
stieh. Ka atiar jong u. ban ialeh pyrshah ia ki Phereng, dei 
ka "Satyagraha", kaba mut, ban bud ne bat skhem ia ka 
nongrim jong ka jingshisha, ka jingialeh pyrshah da ka lynti 
kaba jai jai. khlem umsnam ne khlem pynmynsaw, lehbor 
ne thombor iano iano ruh. U ong ba ka jingialeh ba khlem 





pynmynsaw iano iano kam dei ka atiar jong ki ba tlot ne 
ki ba khawpud, hynrei tang kiba khlain bad ki ba shlur ki 
lah ban iateh ia ka. U Gandhi u la seng ia ka Sabarmati 
Ashram ha Ahmedabad, ha kaba ki paralok bad nongbud 
jong u ki ioh jinghikai, halor ka nortgrim jong ka jingshisha, 
bad khlem pynmynsaw iano iano ruh. 

U Mahatma Gandhi u angnud ia ka Ri India kaba 
laitluid, bad u kwah ban pynbcit, pynthymmai bad pynkylla 
ia ka imlang sahlang jong ka Ri India baroh kawei. Nyngkong 
eh u Gandhi u kwah ban pynkylla ia ka jingim shimet, bad 
ba u wei pa u wei u dei ban kyntiew bad pynbha shuwa 
ia ka jinglong shimet, ban long u bakhuid ha ki jingmut 
jingpyrkhat, ka met ka phad. Kumjuh ruh ban pynlong ia

ka ing ka sem, ka md ka kiar kaba khuid kaba lait na ki 
jaboh jakhlia khnang ban ioh ia ka jingkoit jingkhiah. U 
Gandhi u kwah ruh ba ngin pynneh pynsah ia ka mariang, 
ki khlaw ki btap, ki dieng ki siej bad kiei kiei ki ba don 
ha ka. U ong ba ka mariang ka ai lut ia kiei kiei kiba ngi 
donkam kat kum ka jingdonkam jong ngi, hynrei ym na ka 
bynta ka jingkwah rhah palat jong ngi ne ka jingkhwan 
myntoi jongno jongno ruh. 

U Gandhi u la pynrung ruh ia ka mkom jinghikai skul 
kaba ki khot ka Basic Education. Ka jingthmu ba kongsan 
eh ka long ba, baroh ki jinghikai ha skul, ki dei ban don 
ka nongrim bad jingmyntoi, bad ka jingnang jingstad, ka 
dei ruh ban pynmyntoi ia ka Ri, ha ka imlang sahlang. U 
Gandhi um kwah iaka jinghikai skul, ba la pynrung da ki 
Phareng, tang ban nang thoh bad nangpule, ban nang ia 
ka ktien Phareng bad ban pynmih iaki nongtrei kam sorkar. 

U Gandhi u ong ba, kaba donkam ia ki briew jong ka Ri 
India ka long ban laitluid na ka jingbieit bad sah dum, ba 
ki dei ban sngewthuh, ban trei bad ieng ha ia ki jong ki 
kjat khnang ban long kiba bit ban ioh laitluid bad lah ban 
synshar da lade ia lade. 



Ha ka Hang jong ka jingtrei jingktah, ki kam karkhana 
bad kiwei kiwei de, u Gandhi um kwah ba ngin shaniah 
lut ia Id jingdonkam jong ngi ha ki karkhana Phercng bad 
kiwei kiwei pat. U kwah ba ki nong India, kin lah ban 
pynmih la Id jingdonkam kum ki tiar ki tar, ki jain ki nep 
bad kiwei kiwei de, ha la Ri la jong. U la pyni da ka nuksa

ksai, ka nuksa kaba kongsan tarn eh, bad ha man la ki 
jingialang, dei ban don ka jingthir ksai ryngkat bad ki

jingduwai. 

U Gandhi u pyrshah jur ruh ia kawei na ki jingsniew 
kaba don ha Ri India, kata ka long, ka jingbymbit ktah 
(untouchability) hapdeng ki khra\A^it bad ki ba pohjait. U 
Gandhi u ong ba kane ka dei ka sang bad, ka ain pyrshah

kyntu ba dei ban pdiang ia baroh kumjuh ym ban lehbein 
ia ki ba poh jait da ka jingbymbit ktah ia ki, namar baroh 
ki dei ki khun jong u Blei. 

U Gandhi u kwah ruh, ba ki kynthei ha Ri India, kin 
kiew bad tur shaphrang. Haka por jingialeh laitluid, u 
Gandhi u sngewthuh, ba ka Ri India kan nym lah satia ban 
ioh ia ka jinglaitluid, lada shiteng na ki nongshong shnong, 
kim tip ia kaei kaba ki ialeh. Kumta, ka sur jong u ka par 
sha ka Ri baroh kawei, bad sha ki nongrit nongkyndong, 
bad sha ki kynthei, ki la ia mih ban shim bynta, ha ka 
jingialeh ban ioh ia ka jinglaitluid. 

U Mahatma Gandhi, u thrang bad kwah, ban wan rah 
shibun ki jingkylla ha ka Ri India. U kwah ban pynkylla ia 
ka imlang sahlang, ka rep ka riang, bad kiwei de. U 
Geuidhi u iaiap pyrshah ruh ia ki jingsniew kiba don ha ka 
imlang sahlang kum ka kyiad, kaba long ka jingmyllien 
kaba sniew kaba wan rah ruh shibun ki jingsniew bad 
jingjot jingpra. 



Haba u kren shaphang ka synshar paldbah, u Gandhi 
u ju ban ba ka jingsynshar, kan^ kut tang ha ki khyndiat 
ngut kiba shong ha khlieh, hynrei na Id briew ha man la 
ki nongrit nongkyndong. U Gandhi u kwah ba kiba duk tarn 
kin sngew ba Id don ka sur ban kren., ka India kaba baroh

kin im ha ka suk ka sain. 

Ha ka ba pynkut, ngi shem, ba u Gandhi u long u 
ba iohi lypa bun kiei kiei kiba don ha ka Ri India. U la 
pyni, bad kdew lynti ia kiei kiei kiba dei ban pynkiew ia 
ka imiang sahlang, ka synshar ka khadar, ka ioh ka kot 
bad ka jingnang jingstad. Ki rukom pyrkhat, bad jingiohi 
jong u Gandhi kiba iadei bad ka long briew man briew, id 
dei ki bym don jingkut. Kumta ban kynmaw burom ia u 
Mahatma Gandhi, ngi dei ban iaieh pyrshah ia ki jingsniew 
kiba bun, ha ka imiang sahlang, kum ka ^ngieh thombor, 
ka jingniew bein, ne ban bein ia kiwei pat, ka jingsngewpher 
ka jaid ka kynja, ka niam, ka sniehdoh, ka jingniewpoh, 
ka jingbymbit ktah, ka jingduk ka jingkyrduh ka jingbuaid 
bor bad spah. Ngi dei ban pyniong ia ka Ri India ka jaka 
ba la kheun bha ban shong, bad kaba kiew bad tur shaphrang 
ha kiei kiei baroh. 


Ki rukom pyrkhat jong u Gandhi bad ka 
jingiadei ha ki por mynta 


Ki snem 1919-1948 ki long ki snem jong u Gandhi 
ne 'Gandhi Era' ha ka history jong ka Ri India. Ka jingwan 
rung jong u ha kine ki snem ka la pynkylla ia ka rukom 
ialeh ban ioh ia ka jinglaitluid jong ka Ri India na ki 
Phareng. U la pyndonkam ia ka atiar jong ka 'satyagraha' 
ban ialeh ia ka bor bah ki Phareng. Ym lah ban pynkylla 
thik ia ka ktien 'Satyagraha', la ha ka phareng ne Khasi, 
hynrei ka mut ban bud ne bat skhem ia ka nongrim ka 
jingshisha. Ka 'Satyagraha' ka kynthup ia ka jingialeh lyngba 
ka lynti ka hok, ka jingieit bad ka jingshisha, ka jingialeh 
pyrshcih da ka lynti kaba jai jai, khlem umsnam ne khlem 
pynmynsaw ne lehbor ne thombor iano iano ruh.

U Gandhi u la sdang ia ka 'Non-cooperation' ha ka 
snem 1920 bad ia ka 'Civil Disobedience' ha ka snem 
1930. Ha ka snem 1942, u la sdang ia ka 'Quit India 
Movement' ha kaba pynkhih ba ki Phareng ki dei ban mih 
noh na Ri India. Dei ha ka 30 tarik u Kyllalyngkot ba u 
la shah siat iap ha u Nathuram Godse. 

Ha bun ki khep u Gandhi u la shah kem bad shah 
set patok, la ju khot ieit ia u Bapu, u kpa jong ka 
jaidbynriew. U Gandhi u long u nongpynphriang ia ka 
khubor jong ka jingsuk, ka jingshisha bad ka jingialeh ba 
khlem pynmynsaw iano iano ruh. U ju ong ba u Blei u dei 
ka jingshisha bad ka jingshisha dei u Blei bad u Blei dei 
ka jingieit bad ka jingieit dei u Blei. Ha ka rukom pyrkhat 
jong u Gandhi, ka jingialeh ba khlem pynmynsaw iano iano 
ruh (non-violence) kam dei ka atiar jong kiba tlot ne kiba 
khawpud, hynrei tang kiba khlain bad kiba shlur ki lah ban 
leh ia ka. Ha ka snem 1916, u la seng ia ka Sabarmati 
Ashrzurn ha Ahmedabad, ha kaba ki paralok bad nongbud ■ 



jong u, ki ioh jinghikai ban Ich ia ka nongrim jong ka 
jingshisha bad ka jingialeh ba khiem pynmynsaw iano iano 
nih. Dei ki jinghikai jong ki niam bapher bapher kiba la 
saindur ia ka nikom pyrkhat jong u Gandhi.

Ka Common Wealth bad ka bynta jong ka ha kaba 
Wanrah bad Pynneh ia ka Jingsuk ha ka Pyrthei 

Ki Phareng, kiba la leit kylieng ka pyrthci, khamtam 
ban khaii pateng, kila kylla long, ki nongsynshar kiba khraw 
bor bha, ha bun ki jaka jong ka pyrthei. Kum ka nuksa, 
ki Phareng, kiba ia wan sha Ri India jong ngi, kum ki 
nongkhaii, ha ka snem 1600, suki suki ki la kylla long

pynban ki nongsynshar, haduh ba ngin da ioh ia ka

spah snem, kaba khat khyndai, ki Phareng ki la lah ban 
pynlong ka hima, kaba khraw haduh katta katta, kaba la 
kynthup, kumba shibynta na ka lai bynta jong ka pyrthei, 
bad shi bynta na ka san bynta, jong ki briew ha ka pyrthei. 
Hadien ka thma bah ba nyngkong, ka bor jong ka Ri Bilat, 
kala sdang hiar, bad hadien jong ka thma bah kaba ar ki 
Phareng, ki la sdang ban sngewthuh, ia ka jingdonkam jong 
ka Common Wealth. 

Ka Common Wealth, ka long ka jingiasyllok, jong ki 
Ri laitluid, kiba don mynshwa, hapoh ka hima Bilat, bad 
mynta ki iatrei lang, na ka bynta kino kino ki jingdonkam, 
kiba iadei lang, khnang ban kynticw ia ka jingiasngewthuh 
jingmut, shityllup ka pyrthei. Ka kyrteng Common Wealth, 
la pyndonkam nyngkong eh ha ka Balfour Declaration ha 
u snem 1926. La seng ia ka British Commom wealth, da 
ka Statute of West Minster, ha u snem 1931. Ka Ri 
Australia, New Zealand bad Canada ki dei ki Ri kiba 
nyngkong eh, ban long ki dkhot jong ka British Common 
Wealth of Nations. Hadien ka thma bah kaba ar la khot 
kyrteng noh ka common wealth of Nations namar ka Ri 
India, Pakistan, bad Sri Lanka, ki la kylla long ki Ri kiba 
laitluid. 

Ki Ri, kiba kham paw khmat, kiba don ha ka Common 


wealth mynta, ki long ka Ri Bilat, India, Australia, 
Bangladesh, Canada, Cyprus, Gambia, Ghana, Jamaica, 
Kenya, Malaysia, Malta, Maurituis, New Zealand, Nigeria, 
Singapore, Sri lanka, Swaziland, Tanzania, Trinidad, Tobago, 
Uganda, Zambia, Zimbabwe bad kiwei kiwei de.

Ka Secretariat jong ka Common Wealth, ka don bun 
ki bynta trei kam kum, 

(1) Ban kyntiew bad iatrei lang hapdeng ki Ri jong ka 
Common Wealth, ha ka liang ka ioh ka kot, kaba Iqmthup 
ruh ia ka khaii pateng. 

(2) Ban pynbeit hapdeng Id dkhot jong ka Common Wealth, 
ka kolshor, ka imlang-sahlang kaba kynthup ruh ia ki 
sports, bad ki kam kiba iadei bad ki samla. 

(3) Ban kyntiew, ia ka jingai jinghikai ban pyntbit, haka 
liang ka jingnang jingstad, ka koit ka khiah, ka stad science 
bad ki kam ain. 

(4) Ban ai ka jingiarap ha ki liang Technical, na ka bynta 
ka roi ka par ha ka ioh ka kot,bad ka imlang sahlang, 
lyngba ka Common Wealth Fund for Technical Co-operation. 

(5) Ban pynbeit ia ki jingialang, man la ka snem na ka 
bynta ki minister, kam pisa tyngka, jong ki Ri kiba long 
ki dkhot jong ka Common Wealth. 

(6) Bern pynbeit ia ki jingialang, jong ki khlieh nongsynsheir 
jong ki Ri Common Wealth, Common Wealth Heads of 
Governments (CHOGM).

Ka Common Wealth, ka kwah ban wanrah ia ka 
jingsuk, bad ka shongsuk shong shngain ha ka pyrthei 
baroh kawei. Ka Conference kaba b long ha Ottawa ha 
u snem 1973, ka la pidiang ia ka rai, ban kyrpad b kito

Ka Common wealth, ka kwah ruh, ban ai kti lang

shibun ka umphnieing, ka Punjab shibun u khaw, bad ka

kaba kiwei ki jyiia ki don, kiwei pat kim don, namaikata 
ngi donkam ka jingiaai bad jingiashim mar kylliang, kaba, 
pyni ba ngi donkam iwei ia iwei pat, bad ngim lah ban 
im tang marwei. Kumta, ia ha ka khaii ka pateng, ka kA 
ka kot ne ka imieuig sahiang ngi donkam marlqrlliai^. 

Ka jingsuk bad ka jingialong paralok, ka wan rah ia 
ka jingroi jingpar, hynrei kano kano ka jinglehbor, ka wan 
rah ia ka jingjot jingpra, bad sah dien ha bun ki Iiang. Kane 
ka dei ka juk jong ka Science bad Technology, ka ^ik jong 
ka jingialeh, ban tur bad kiew shaphrang, namar tang Idia 
kiba tbit tarn ki lah ban kiew sha khlieh. 


Ngim lah ban ioh ia ka jingshong suk shongsain, iada 
ngi dang don ka jingsngew, kaba ki ong, ka khmat namar 
ka khmat bad ka bniat namar ka bniat. U Mahatma 
Gandhi uba long u kpa jong ka jaidbynriew bad u ba la 
ialeh ban ioh ia ka jinglaltluid na ka jingsynshar ki Phareng 
da ka lynti ba jai jai khlem pynmynsaw iano iano ruh (ne 
ka non-violence) 

K jingma ba khraw tarn jong ka Ri, Id long ka ^g 
khim mynsiem, ka jingsngewpher ka ktien ka thylliej, ka

bad ki jingpyntriem. Lada ym lah ban pynduh ia kine, bad 
wanrah ia ka jingiatylli ha ka Ri, ngin sa hap ha shibun 
ki jingjynjar bad jingeh. Ka jingisih kan nym lah ban pynduh 
ia ka jingisih, dei tang ka jingieit kaba lah ban leh. Ka 
Prime Minister barim, ka Smt. Indira Gandhi, ka ong, ba, 
ka pyrthei kan lait im, tang lada ngi iehnoh ia ka jingialeh 
pyrshah, bad rat dyngkhong ia ka jingtieng, ka jingisih bad

ka jingsngew bym iaryngkat. 

Kano kano ka jaidbynriew, kan nym lah ban roi ne 
neh slem khlem ka jingsuk, ka jingieit ka jingiatylli bad ka 
jingsngew ne jinglong paralok. Kumta, kaba don ha khmat 
jong ngi, ka long, ba ngin jied ia ka jingiatylli ne ka jingjot 
jingpra, bad ia ka jingiatylli ne jingsngewlong kawei, kumba 
ong u Dr. S. Radhakrishnan, u President ba mynshuwa jong 
ka Ri India, ym lah ban tei da kaei kaei ruh, hynrei ka 
dei ban wan ha ka jingmut, jingpyrkhat bad ka dohnud jong 
ki briew. 

Ngi dei ban trei ban wan rah ia ka jingiatylli ha ka 
Ri India jong ngi, ban sngew ba, hapdeng ki jingiapher kiba 
bun ha ka Ri, hynrei ngi ia long hapoh kawei ka Ri. 
Kumta, ka Ri jong ngi, ka lah ban tur shaphrang ha baroh 
ki Hang, ha ka imlang sahlang, ka ioh ka kot. ne ka 
jingnang jingstad.

ki thaw jong ngi, namar ki iing ki sem kiba bha, ki pynlong

ia ki shnong kiba bha, bad ki shnong kiba bha ki pynlong 
ia ka Ri kaba bha, bad ruh ia ka pyrthei kaba bha. Shuwa

Ka Jingsuk Ka Long Kaba Donkam Eh Na Ka 
Bynta Ka Jingroi Ka Jingpar 


Ka jingsuk ka long kaei kaei kaba donkam eh na ka 
bynta ka roi ka par, jong kano kano ka jaidbynriew, bad 
ka Ri. Haba don ka jingsuk, hangta don ka jingieit ka 
jingkmen bad ka jingroi ka jingpar. Ha shuwa ba ngin 
pynkhreh ia ka spah snem ba arphew wei ngi dei ban 
pynkhreh iaiade ban don ka jingsuk, ka jingieid. U Jisu 
Krist, u la ong 'suk kiba pyniasuk, naba yn khot ia ki, ki 
khun jong u Blei (Matthew 5:9). 

Ka Mother Teresa kaba la trei hapdeng kiba duk na 
kiba duk tarn ka apostol jong ka jingsuk bad ka ba la ioh 
pdiang ia ka Nobel Peace Prize kala ong, ban ioh ia ka 
jingsuk, ngi dei ban pyrshang ban kren kham bun ia u Blei 
bad ban don bad u Blei, ngin ialap ia ka jingsuk jong u 
Khrist ha kiei kiei kiba ngi leh, kumba u Jisu u la leit laid 
kylleng ban leh bha. 

Baroh ki niam ki hikai ban don ka jingieit bad ka 
jingsuk. Kumta, ki jaidbynriew ha ka pyrthei ki dei ban 
nang ban im ha ka jingsuk, ban kiew ha ka jingnang ka 
jingstad, ka thoh ka pule,ka imlang ka sahlang ka roi ka 
par, ha ki ing ki sem, ka jylla, bad ka Ri hi baroh kawei.

U Pandit JaweJiarlal Nehru u myntri rangbeJi duh jong 
ka Ri India, u la ong, ba ka jingsuk kam dei tang ban nym 
ia leh thma, hynrei ka jingangnud jong ngi ban wanrah ia 
ka jinglong kaba suk na ka bynta ka jingsuk. (Peace in the 
words of Jawaharlal Nehru is not merely a physical abstention 
from war, it is a desire to create a climate of peace for 
peace). U John Milton u ong, ka jingjop jong ka jingsuk 
kam shym duna na ka jingjop jong ka thma. (Peace hath 


her victories no less theui that of war). Ka jingsuk ka dei 
ka jinglong kaba laitluid na ka thma, ki ^gma, jingtriem, 
bad jingpynmynsaw bad ka jingim ha ka jingsuk jingsain. 

Ki samla ki don ka bynta kaba khraw tarn ha ka ban 
wanrah ia ka suk ka sain ha ka imiang sahlang. Ki samla 
ki dei ban long ki nongpynphriang ia ka jingsuk ha ki ing 
ki sem, ki shnong ki thaw, ha ka jylla la jong bad ka Ri 
hi baroh kawei. 


Ha kine ki por, ngi iohi shibun kiei Wei ki jingkylla 
kiba wan ha ka imiang sahlang, ka ioh ka kot bad ka 
synshar khadar. Ka jingiaid stet jong ka Science bad 
Technology ka la pynkylla lut ia Wei Wei baroh bad teng 
teng ngim sngewthuh bad pei phang shuh. Man la ka sngi, 
haba ngi pule ia W kot khubor ngi iohi ka jingpyniap, ka 
tuh ka thiem, ka bamsap, W drugs, ka jingpyntieng pyntriem, 
ka jingkwah spah da kaba suk, bad kwah palat ia Wei Wei 
W jong ka pyrthei bad kiwei Wwei de W jingsniew ha ka 
imiang sahlang. Kine baroh W wanrah ia ka jingticng bad 
ka jingbymdon ka shongsuk shong shngain namar ngirn tip 
aiu kan jia ha kano kano ka por,ne ha kano kano ka jaka, 
bad ka la kylla long kum ka jingpang ba iabit, bad lada 
ngim leh eiei mynta, kan poi ka por ba ngin nym lah shuh

ban pynduh. Ka pisa kaba dei ban pynlut ia ka jingroi 
jingpar, la pynlut ha ka law & order. 


Ki samla W dei jong ka Ri bad ka jaidbynn^. W 
dei ruh W budlum jong ka Ri. bad ka jaidbynriew. Ka Ri 
ka donkam ki samla kiba tbit ha baroh ki Hang. Ngi 
donkam ka jingstad bad ka jabieng. khnang te ngm lait ria 
kino Wno W jingshah lehbein bad shal. ban tern. ^ 

W dei ban long Wba minot bad trei shitom bad roh ia Wei 
Wei da ka jingtrel shitom. Ki dei ban long Wte teh lakam 
ialade (discipline). Kano kano ka Ri bad ka jaidbsmnew k^ 
nym lah satia ban kiew shaphrang lada kam don la 
discipline bad ki briew ki. long kiba adkar ha kiei kiei 


baroh. Ngim lah satia ban im tang da ka spah, ki iing paki 
dubn, hvnrei da ka jinglong babha jong ki briew. Ki samla 
ki dei ban im ka jingim kaba bha ban aiti, ban iarap, len 
ialade. Ngin ioh ia ka jingsuk lada kloi ban duh ia kiei kiei 
baroh, bad lait na ka jingbishni jingpihuin. U briew u ba 
kwah lut, un nym lah satia ban ioh jingsuk. Teng teng ngi 
pyrkhat ba ka spah bad ka jingioh lut ia kiei kiei kan 
wanrah ia ka jingsuk. Hynrei don ki iing kiba duk hynrei 
ki don ka jingsuk, don pat ki iing kiba riewspah kim don 
saha ka jingsuk. mynta, bun na ngi, ngi beh ia ka spah 
bad ka rong phong, bad ngi klet noh ia kiei kiei kiba kynja 
mynsiem. Ki briew kl la tip bad shakri ia u Blei, kiba 
tieng ia u Blei bad kiba ju duwai bad shaniah ha u kin ioh 
ia ka jingsuk. U briew u ba khuid ka mynsiem, u dei u 
briew u ba suk. Ki briew kiba bam sap, kiba thok, shukor 
kim lah satia ban ioh ia ka jingsuk. 

Ban wanrah ia ka jingsuk bad ka shongsuk shong

shngain, baroh ngi don ka bynta ban leh eiei. Ka long kaei 
kaei ka bym sngew dei, ba ngin khmih lynti ba kiwei kin 
leh na ka bynta jong ngi, lada meuigi hi ngim sdang. Ki 
^ntap shipai, ki pulit, ki ain ki kanun kiba eh, kin nym 
lah ban leh eiei, lada ka jinglq/Ua, kam wetn na ngi, na ki 
dohnud jong ngi, na lade hi, na ki shnong ki thaw, bad 
ka jyila hi baroh kawei. 

Kumta ngi dei ban sdang shuwa na lade, kumba don 
ka jingong, lada ngi kwah ban pynkylla ia ka pyrthei ngi 
dei ban pynkylla shuwa ia lade nangta, na ki ing ki sem 
jong ngi, ia ki shnong ki thaw jong ngi ia ka nongbah jong 
ngi, ia ka jyila jong ngi bad i? ka Ri baroh kawei, kaei kaba 
ngi lah ban leh shimet shimet, Utne manvei marwei. Ngi 
dei ban long kiba sbun, Idba trei shitom, kiba lait na ka 
^gbishni, pihuin, khim mynsiem, bad ngin trei ban wanrah 
ia ka jingsuk ka jingsain. Ki ing ki sem jong ngi ki dei bein 
lait na ka jingiamai, iania bad kiba shong suk shong sain. 




iing ka dci ban long ka jaka kaba lah ban ai jingiarap, 
jingkyrshan, ka jingtngen, ka jingsuk ia ki khun. Namar 
kaei kaei kaba jia bad long ha iing, ka ktah ia ki khun. 
Ka jingbuaid, jingpait jingpra, Id ing ki sem, ka jingiamai 
iania, ka jingshah lehbein ka kmie, ki ktah jur ia ki khun. 

Ki samla ki dei ban hikai iwei ia iwei pat ban im ka 
jingim kaba bha, ban lait na ki kam kiba sniew, ngi dci 
ban iada hi da lade ia lade, lyngba ki ing ki sem ki shnong 
ki thaw jong ngi. Ki paidbah ki dei ban sngewthuh ba ki 
kam sniew, bad pynmynsaw kaba ki leh da katto katne 
ngut ki briew, kan pyntroin ia ka jaidbynriew, ia ka ioh 
ka kot, ka imlang sahlang bad ngin long kiba sahdien. Ki

samla ki dei ban pynkylla iaki jingmut jingpyrkhat, bad ki 
dohnud jongki kin ai jingsuk ia ki bym don jingsuk, ka 
jingkyrmen ia kiba duh jingkyrmen, ka jingieit ia ki bym 
don jingieit. 

Ka jingsuk ka long kaei kaei kaba donkam eh, namar 
ia kaei kaei kaba la pynsniew, kan shim por da ki phew 
snem ban pynkylla pat. Haba ngin sa kynjoh sha ka 2000 
snem jong ka jingwan u Krist bad ba ngin kynjoh ruh sha 
ka spah snem kaba arphewwei, ai ba maphi ki samla phin 
ia kut jingmut skhem ban wanrah ia ka jingsuk bad ka 
shongsuk shongsain, ka roi ka par ha ka jvlla jong ngi, bad 
ka Ri baroh kawei.
